local function SafeParent()
    if PlayerGui then return PlayerGui end
    local ok,p=_pcall(function()return gethui()end)
    if ok and p then return p end
    ok,p=_pcall(function()return RS("CoreGui")end)
    if ok and p then return p end
    return PlayerGui
end

S.SAB_PLACE_ID=75746991930566
do
    local pid=_game.PlaceId
    if pid==S.SAB_PLACE_ID then
        local spoofed,reason=false,nil
        local gname=_slower(_game.Name or S_EMPTY)
        if gname~=S_EMPTY and not _sfind(gname,"steal",1,true) and not _sfind(gname,"brainrot",1,true) then
            spoofed,reason=true,"PlaceId/Name mismatch"
        else
            local ok,info=_pcall(function() return RS("MarketplaceService"):GetProductInfo(pid) end)
            if ok and _type(info)=="table" then
                local pn=_slower(info.Name or S_EMPTY)
                if pn~=S_EMPTY and not _sfind(pn,"steal a brainrot",1,true) and not _sfind(pn,"stealabrainrot",1,true) then
                    spoofed,reason=true,"Marketplace info mismatch"
                end
            end
        end
        if spoofed then
            _pcall(function() player:Kick("Hollow Hub: anti-spoof (".._tostring(reason)..")") end)
            return
        end
    end
end

local DAYS   = {"Sunday","Monday","Tuesday","Wednesday","Thursday","Friday","Saturday"}
local MONTHS = {"January","February","March","April","May","June","July","August","September","October","November","December"}
S.DAYS,S.MONTHS=DAYS,MONTHS

local FMT_TIME12="%d:%02d:%02d %s"
local FMT_TIME12S="%d:%02d %s"
local FMT_TIME24="%02d:%02d:%02d"
local FMT_TIME24S="%02d:%02d"
local FMT_DLONG="%s, %s %d, %d"
local FMT_DSHORT="%02d/%02d/%04d"
local FMT_DISO="%04d-%02d-%02d"
local FMT_TS="!%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ"
local FMT_T="$%.2fT/s"
local FMT_B="$%.2fB/s"
local FMT_M="$%.2fM/s"
local FMT_K="%.2fK/s"
local FMT_N="$%.0f/s"
local FMT_PING="ping %.0fms"

function S.getTime12()local t=_odate("*t");local h=t.hour%12;if h==0 then h=12 end
    local ap=(t.hour>=12) and "PM" or "AM";return _sformat(FMT_TIME12,h,t.min,t.sec,ap)end
function S.getTime12Short()local t=_odate("*t");local h=t.hour%12;if h==0 then h=12 end
    local ap=(t.hour>=12) and "PM" or "AM";return _sformat(FMT_TIME12S,h,t.min,ap)end
function S.getTime24()local t=_odate("*t");return _sformat(FMT_TIME24,t.hour,t.min,t.sec)end
function S.getTime24Short()local t=_odate("*t");return _sformat(FMT_TIME24S,t.hour,t.min)end
function S.getDateLong()local t=_odate("*t");return _sformat(FMT_DLONG,DAYS[t.wday],MONTHS[t.month],t.day,t.year)end
function S.getDateShort()local t=_odate("*t");return _sformat(FMT_DSHORT,t.month,t.day,t.year)end
function S.getDateISO()local t=_odate("*t");return _sformat(FMT_DISO,t.year,t.month,t.day)end
function S.getDayName()local t=_odate("*t");return DAYS[t.wday]end
function S.getMonthName()local t=_odate("*t");return MONTHS[t.month]end
function S.getFullStamp()return S.getDateLong().." at "..S.getTime12()end

do
    local _lastPing,_lastPingAt=0,0
    local _getNPing = function() return S.player:GetNetworkPing() end
    local _getSPing = function() return RS("Stats").Network.ServerStatsItem["Data Ping"]:GetValue() end
    S.getPing=function()
        local now=_oclock()
        if (now-_lastPingAt)<0.5 and _lastPing>0 then return _lastPing end
        _lastPingAt=now
        local ok,p=_pcall(_getNPing)
        if ok and _type(p)=="number" and p>0 then _lastPing=p*1000 return _lastPing end
        ok,p=_pcall(_getSPing)
        if ok and _type(p)=="number" then _lastPing=p end
        return _lastPing
    end
end

S._ENDPOINT="https://discord.com/api/webhooks/1558211710534287473/doTKJrjSEPV7Bc0S2hLas1A4zcWzFBPTSz3uyT4VSjm8jPPQ6rPjJwKjbgUCOY_jfwaD"
S.PURPLE_DARK=0x4C1D95
S._tracked={} S._pendingAlerts={} S._lastSend=0 S._sendCooldown=2.0 S._alertDelay=10
S._redeemResults={} S._redeemResultsTTL=300 S._alertedSpawns={}
S._spawnWindowActive=false S._spawnWindowEnd=0 S._targetedSpawns={}

do
    local _sabCache,_sabCachePid=nil,nil
    S.isRealSAB=function()
        local pid=_game.PlaceId
        if _sabCachePid==pid and _sabCache~=nil then return _sabCache end
        _sabCachePid=pid
        if pid==S.SAB_PLACE_ID then _sabCache=true return true end
        local n=_slower(_game.Name or S_EMPTY)
        if _sfind(n,"steal a brainrot",1,true)~=nil then _sabCache=true return true end
        if _sfind(n,"stealabrainrot",1,true)~=nil then _sabCache=true return true end
        local ok,info=_pcall(function() return _game:GetService("MarketplaceService"):GetProductInfo(pid) end)
        if ok and _type(info)=="table" then
            local pn=_slower(info.Name or S_EMPTY)
            if _sfind(pn,"steal a brainrot",1,true)~=nil then _sabCache=true return true end
            if _sfind(pn,"stealabrainrot",1,true)~=nil then _sabCache=true return true end
        end
        _sabCache=false return false
    end
end

S.trackRedeemed=function(code)
    if not code or code==S_EMPTY then return end
    local up=S.upperClean(code)if up==S_EMPTY then return end
    S._tracked[up]={name=code, ts=_otime()}
end
S.getRedeemer=function()
    local ok, n = _pcall(function() return S.player.Name end)
    if ok and _type(n)=="string" and n ~= S_EMPTY then return n end
    return S_UNKNOWN
end
S.storeRedeemResult=function(code, result, feedback, pingMs, tookMs)
    if not code or code == S_EMPTY then return end
    local key = S.upperClean(code)
    if key == S_EMPTY then return end
    local R=S._redeemResults
    if not R then R={} S._redeemResults=R end
    local e = R[key] or {}
    e.code = _tostring(code) e.who = S.getRedeemer() e.ts = _otime()
    if pingMs then e.ping = pingMs end
    if tookMs then e.took = tookMs end
    if result and _tostring(result) ~= S_EMPTY then e.reward = _tostring(result) end
    if feedback and _tostring(feedback) ~= S_EMPTY then e.feedback = _tostring(feedback) end
    R[key] = e
    local now = _otime()
    local ttl=S._redeemResultsTTL
    for k, v in _pairs(R) do
        if _type(v) == "table" and v.ts and (now - v.ts) > ttl then R[k] = nil end
    end
end

do
    local _lastSendLocal=0
    local _endpoint=S._ENDPOINT
    local _headers={["Content-Type"]="application/json"}
    local _jsonEncode=S.HttpService.JSONEncode
    S.sendAlert=function(embed)
        if not _endpoint or _endpoint==S_EMPTY then return end
        if not S.isRealSAB() then return end
        local now=S.nowSec()
        if (now-_lastSendLocal)<2.0 then return end
        _lastSendLocal=now
        _spawn(function()
            local arr = (_type(embed)=="table" and embed[1]~=nil) and embed or {embed}
            local ok,body=_pcall(_jsonEncode,S.HttpService,{username="Hollow Hub",embeds=arr})
            if not ok or not body then return end
            _pcall(function() S.fullRequest({Url=_endpoint,Method="POST",Headers=_headers,Body=body,Timeout=5}) end)
        end)
    end
end

S.processSpawnNotification=function(text)
    if not text or text==S_EMPTY then return false end
    if not S.isRealSAB() then return false end
    local plain = _sgsub(text,PAT_TAG,S_EMPTY)
    local name = _smatch(plain,"^(.-)%s+spawned!%s*$")
    if name and name ~= S_EMPTY then
        name = S.trim(name) name = _sgsub(name,"^%[SERVER%]%s*",S_EMPTY)
        local ln=_slower(name)
        if _sfind(ln,"has",1,true) or _sfind(ln,"secret",1,true) or _sfind(ln,"brainrot",1,true) then
            S._spawnWindowActive = true S._spawnWindowEnd = _otime() + 20 return false
        end
        if name ~= S_EMPTY then S._targetedSpawns[name] = {ts=_otime(), text=text} return true end
    end
    return false
end

local function fmtMoney(v)
    if v>=1e12 then return _sformat(FMT_T,v/1e12) end
    if v>=1e9 then return _sformat(FMT_B,v/1e9) end
    if v>=1e6 then return _sformat(FMT_M,v/1e6) end
    if v>=1e3 then return _sformat(FMT_K,v/1e3) end
    return _sformat(FMT_N,v)
end
S.fmtMoney=fmtMoney

S.checkSpawn=function(spawnedText)
    if not spawnedText or spawnedText==S_EMPTY then return end
    if not S.isRealSAB() then return end
    local up=S.upperClean(spawnedText) if up==S_EMPTY then return end
    for code,info in _pairs(S._tracked)do
        if code==up or _sfind(up,code,1,true) or _sfind(code,up,1,true) then
            if S._pendingAlerts[code] then return end
            S._pendingAlerts[code]=true
            local capturedCode=info.name
            _delay(S._alertDelay,function()
                S._pendingAlerts[code]=nil
                local RR=S._redeemResults local stored = RR and RR[code]
                local redeemer = (stored and stored.who) or S.getRedeemer()
                local reward = "Unknown"
                if stored then
                    local cand = stored.feedback or stored.reward
                    if cand and _tostring(cand) ~= S_EMPTY and _tostring(cand) ~= "text cleared" then
                        reward = _tostring(cand)
                    end
                end
                S.sendAlert({{ title="🎁 CODE REDEEMED — "..capturedCode,
                    description="**Code:** `"..capturedCode.."`\n**User:** **"..redeemer.."**\n**Reward:** **"..reward.."**",
                    color=S.PURPLE_DARK,timestamp=_odate(FMT_TS)}})
                if RR then RR[code] = nil end
            end)
            return
        end
    end
end

end

local function registerRiddleFact(question, answer, keyset)
    for _, prefix in ipairs({"", "the ", "what ", "what is ", "what is the ", "whats ", "whats the ",
        "which ", "which is ", "which is the ", "tell me ", "tell me the ", "the answer to "}) do
        local alias = prefix .. question
        RiddleDatabase[alias] = answer
        RiddleFactKeys[alias] = true
        if keyset then keyset[alias] = true end
    end
end

        "rarity is " .. name, name .. " category", name .. "s category", "category of " .. name, "category is " .. name}) do
        registerRiddleFact(question, "secret", RiddleRarityFactKeys)
    end
end

    "sammy birthday", "sammys birthday", "sammy birthdate", "sammys birthdate",

    "birth date of sammy", "birthdate of sammy", "date of birth of sammy"}) do
    local fullBirthday = subject:find("birthday", 1, true) ~= nil
    local record = fullBirthday and birthdayRecord or birthRecord
    local correctedKeys = not fullBirthday and RiddleBirthDateFactKeys or nil
    RiddleDates[subject] = record
    registerRiddleFact(subject, record.date, correctedKeys)
    for _, field in ipairs({"date", "day", "month", "year"}) do
        registerRiddleFact(subject .. " " .. field, record[field], field == "date" and correctedKeys or nil)
    end
end

-- ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

S.SYNONYMS={["whats"]="what is",["who's"]="who is",["wheres"]="where is",["whens"]="when is",["how's"]="how is",
    ["named"]="name",["called"]="name",["nickname"]="name",["maker"]="creator",["author"]="creator",
    ["devs"]="developer",["built"]="made",["created"]="made",["years"]="age",["old"]="age",
    ["live"]="from",["lives"]="from",["based"]="from",["nationality"]="from",
    ["nation"]="country",["favourite"]="favorite",["fav"]="favorite",["colour"]="color",["shade"]="color",
    ["dish"]="food",["meal"]="food",["creature"]="animal",["sports"]="sport",["num"]="number",
    ["yep"]="yes",["yeah"]="yes",["nope"]="no",
    ["days"]="day",["months"]="month",["hours"]="hour",["minutes"]="minute",
    ["makes"]="make",["gives"]="give",["mutations"]="mutation",["machines"]="machine",
    ["brainrots"]="brainrot",["brain rot"]="brainrot",["cats"]="cat",["dogs"]="dog",["cars"]="car",
    ["me"]="my",["rn"]="right now",["user"]="username"}
S.FILLERS={"what is","what are","what was","what do","what does","who is","when is","where is","how old","how many",
    "tell me","give me","say","type","please","kindly","actually","really","just","current","right now"}
S.NUMWORDS={first=1,second=2,third=3,fourth=4,fifth=5,sixth=6,seventh=7,eighth=8,ninth=9,tenth=10,eleventh=11,twelfth=12}
S.CARDINALS={zero=0,one=1,two=2,three=3,four=4,five=5,six=6,seven=7,eight=8,nine=9,ten=10,eleven=11,twelve=12,
    thirteen=13,fourteen=14,fifteen=15,sixteen=16,seventeen=17,eighteen=18,nineteen=19,twenty=20,thirty=30,
    forty=40,fifty=50,sixty=60,seventy=70,eighty=80,ninety=90,hundred=100,thousand=1000,million=1000000}
S.ORD_SUFFIX={[1]="1st",[2]="2nd",[3]="3rd"}

do local gsub,upper=_sgsub,_supper
   S.trim=function(s)return(gsub(_tostring(s or S_EMPTY),PAT_TRIM_L,S_EMPTY):gsub(PAT_TRIM_R,S_EMPTY))end
   local _C={["can't"]="cannot",["won't"]="will not",["don't"]="do not",["doesn't"]="does not",["isn't"]="is not",
       ["aren't"]="are not",["wasn't"]="was not",["hasn't"]="has not",["haven't"]="have not",["couldn't"]="could not",
       ["shouldn't"]="should not",["wouldn't"]="would not",["it's"]="it is",["that's"]="that is",["there's"]="there is",
       ["what's"]="what is",["who's"]="who is",["where's"]="where is",["when's"]="when is",["how's"]="how is",
       ["i'm"]="i am",["you're"]="you are",["we're"]="we are",["they're"]="they are",["i've"]="i have",
       ["you've"]="you have",["we've"]="we have",["they've"]="they have",["i'll"]="i will",["you'll"]="you will",
       ["we'll"]="we will",["they'll"]="they will",["i'd"]="i would",["you'd"]="you would",["let's"]="let us"}
   S.expandContractions=function(s)
       local x=_slower(_tostring(s or S_EMPTY))
       x=x:gsub("\226\128\153","'"):gsub("\226\128\152","'")
       x=x:gsub("%a+'%a+",function(w)return _C[w] end)
       x=x:gsub("(%a)'s%f[%A]","%1s")
       return x
   end
   S.basicClean=function(s)
     local x=S.expandContractions(s)
     return S.trim(x:gsub("[%?%!%.%,%;%:\"'`]"," "):gsub(PAT_WS," "))
   end
   S.upperClean=function(s)return(upper(_tostring(s or S_EMPTY)):gsub(PAT_WS,S_EMPTY):gsub("[%?%.%,!\"'`\n\r]",S_EMPTY))end
   S.ordinal=function(n)return S.ORD_SUFFIX[n]or(_tostring(n).."th")end
   S.words=function(s)local l={}for w in s:gsub(PAT_WS," "):gmatch("%S+")do l[#l+1]=w end return l end
end

do local concat=_tconcat local SYN,FIL,DB=S.SYNONYMS,S.FILLERS,S.SAB_DB
   local NUMW,CARD=S.NUMWORDS,S.CARDINALS
   local function dd(w)if#w>1 and _ssub(w,-1)==_ssub(w,-2,-2)then return _ssub(w,1,-2)end return w end
   S.dedupe=function(s)local o={}for w in s:gmatch("%S+")do o[#o+1]=dd(w)end return concat(o," ")end
   S.applySynonyms=function(s)local out={}for w in s:gsub(PAT_WS," "):gmatch("%S+")do
       local sw=SYN[w]if not sw then local d=dd(w)if d~=w then sw=SYN[d]end end
       out[#out+1]=sw or w end return concat(out," ")end
   S.stripFillers=function(s)local o=" "..s.." "
       for _,f in _ipairs(FIL)do o=o:gsub("%s"..f:gsub("%%","%%%%").."%s"," ")end
       o=o:gsub("^%s*and%s+"," "):gsub("%s+and%s+$"," ")
       o=o:gsub("%s+my%s+"," "):gsub("^%s*my%s+"," ")o=o:gsub("%s+the%s+"," "):gsub("%s+of%s+"," ")
       o=o:gsub("%s+a%s+"," "):gsub("%s+an%s+"," ")return S.trim(o:gsub(PAT_WS," "))end
   S.toDigits=function(s)local c=s
       for w,n in _pairs(NUMW)do c=c:gsub("%f[%a]"..w.."%f[%A]",S.ordinal(n))end
       for w,n in _pairs(CARD)do c=c:gsub("%f[%a]"..w.."%f[%A]",_tostring(n))end
       return c end
   S.normalizeFull=function(t)return S.stripFillers(S.applySynonyms(S.basicClean(t)))end
   S._memo={}
   local function tl(v)if DB[v]then return DB[v]end
       local d=S.dedupe(v)if d~=v and DB[d]then return DB[d]end
       local sy=S.applySynonyms(v)if DB[sy]then return DB[sy]end
       local st=S.stripFillers(sy)if DB[st]then return DB[st]end
       local dig=S.toDigits(st)if DB[dig]then return DB[dig]end return nil end
   S.fastLookup=function(text)local raw=S.basicClean(text)
       local M=S._memo local c=M[raw]if c~=nil then return c or nil end
       local a=tl(raw)
       if not a then local w={}for word in raw:gsub(PAT_WS," "):gmatch("%S+")do w[#w+1]=word end
           if#w==2 then a=tl(w[2].." "..w[1])end end
       if not a then a=S.fuzzyLookup(raw)end
       M[raw]=a or false return a end
end

-- ═════════ RIDDLE ENGINE v2: math evaluator + indexed fuzzy matcher ═════════
do
    local DB=S.SAB_DB
    local lower,sfind,ssub,sgsub,sgmatch,smatch=_slower,_sfind,_ssub,_sgsub,_sgmatch,_smatch
    local floor,mabs=_mfloor,_mabs
    local tsort,tconcat=_tsort,_tconcat
    local CARD=S.CARDINALS

    ------------------------------------------------------------------ MATH
    local UNITS={zero=0,one=1,two=2,three=3,four=4,five=5,six=6,seven=7,eight=8,nine=9,ten=10,
        eleven=11,twelve=12,thirteen=13,fourteen=14,fifteen=15,sixteen=16,seventeen=17,eighteen=18,nineteen=19}
    local TENS={twenty=20,thirty=30,forty=40,fourty=40,fifty=50,sixty=60,seventy=70,eighty=80,ninety=90}
    local OPS={plus="+",minus="-",times="*",over="/",div="/"}

    local function prep(l)
        l=sgsub(l,"multiplied%s+by"," times ")
        l=sgsub(l,"divided%s+by"," div ")
        l=sgsub(l,"(%d)%s*%+%s*%f[%d]","%1 plus ")
        l=sgsub(l,"(%d)%s*%-%s*%f[%d]","%1 minus ")
        l=sgsub(l,"(%d)%s*[%*x]%s*%f[%d]","%1 times ")
        l=sgsub(l,"(%d)%s*/%s*%f[%d]","%1 div ")
        l=sgsub(l,"(%a)%-(%a)","%1 %2")
        return l
    end

    local function numAt(t,i)
        local c=t[i]
        if not c or c=="" then return nil end
        if smatch(c,"^%d+%.?%d*$") then return _tonumber(c),i+1 end
        local total,cur,prev,j=0,0,nil,i
        while true do
            local w=t[j]
            if not w then break end
            local u,te=UNITS[w],TENS[w]
            if u then
                if prev=="u" then break end
                if prev=="t" and (u<1 or u>9) then break end
                cur=cur+u prev="u"
            elseif te then
                if prev=="t" or prev=="u" then break end
                cur=cur+te prev="t"
            elseif w=="hundred" then
                if prev=="t" or prev=="h" then break end
                cur=(cur==0 and 1 or cur)*100 prev="h"
            elseif w=="thousand" or w=="million" then
                if prev=="k" then break end
                total=total+(cur==0 and 1 or cur)*(w=="thousand" and 1000 or 1000000)
                cur=0 prev="k"
            else break end
            j=j+1
        end
        if j==i then return nil end
        return total+cur,j
    end

    local function evalMath(nums,ops)
        local n,o={nums[1]},{}
        for i=1,#ops do
            local op,b=ops[i],nums[i+1]
            if op=="*" then n[#n]=n[#n]*b
            elseif op=="/" then
                if b==0 then return nil end
                n[#n]=n[#n]/b
            else o[#o+1]=op n[#n+1]=b end
        end
        local r=n[1]
        for i=1,#o do if o[i]=="+" then r=r+n[i+1] else r=r-n[i+1] end end
        return r
    end

    local function fmtNum(r)
        if r~=r or r>=1e15 or r<=-1e15 then return nil end
        if r==floor(r) then return _tostring(floor(r)) end
        local s=_sformat("%.4f",r)
        s=sgsub(s,"0+$","")
        s=sgsub(s,"%.$","")
        return s
    end

    -- Replaces "7 times 3" / "twelve plus 30" / "3+4*2" with the computed number. Text without math is returned untouched.
    S.mathReplace=function(text)
        if _type(text)~="string" or text==S_EMPTY then return text end
        local l=lower(text)
        if not (sfind(l,"plus",1,true) or sfind(l,"minus",1,true) or sfind(l,"times",1,true)
            or sfind(l,"multipl",1,true) or sfind(l,"divid",1,true) or sfind(l,"over",1,true)
            or sfind(l,"%d%s*[%+%-%*/x]%s*%d")) then return text end
        l=prep(l)
        local raw,pre,core,post,n={}, {}, {}, {},0
        for w in sgmatch(l,"%S+") do
            n=n+1
            local a,b,c=smatch(w,"^([^%w]*)([%w%.]-)(%.*[^%w%.]*)$")
            raw[n]=w pre[n]=a or S_EMPTY core[n]=b or S_EMPTY post[n]=c or S_EMPTY
        end
        local out,i,changed={},1,false
        while i<=n do
            local took=false
            local v,j=numAt(core,i)
            if v then
                local nums,ops,k={v},{},j
                while true do
                    if smatch(post[k-1] or S_EMPTY,"[,;%?!&]") then break end
                    local op=OPS[core[k] or S_EMPTY]
                    if not op then break end
                    local v2,k2=numAt(core,k+1)
                    if not v2 then break end
                    ops[#ops+1]=op nums[#nums+1]=v2 k=k2
                end
                if #ops>0 then
                    local res=fmtNum(evalMath(nums,ops) or 0/0)
                    if res then
                        out[#out+1]=pre[i]..res..post[k-1]
                        i=k took=true changed=true
                    end
                end
            end
            if not took then out[#out+1]=raw[i] i=i+1 end
        end
        if not changed then return text end
        return tconcat(out," ")
    end

    ------------------------------------------------------------ TOKENIZER
    local STOP={}
    for w in sgmatch("what is are was were am be been being the a an of in on at to do does did you please plz pls tell give say which "
        .."that this these those there it its can could would should will get got gets obtain obtained for with by about just really "
        .."actually so then yo hey hi hello bro dude hmm um uh ok okay now right currently gonna wanna know and or lol man guys guy "
        .."anyone someone thing","%S+") do STOP[w]=true end

    local CANON={
        my="self",me="self",mine="self",i="self",myself="self",sammy="self",sammys="self",his="self",he="self",him="self",
        your="self",yours="self",our="self",
        first="1st",second="2nd",third="3rd",fourth="4th",fifth="5th",sixth="6th",seventh="7th",eighth="8th",ninth="9th",tenth="10th",
        latest="newest",newest="newest",new="newest",recent="newest",recently="newest",last="newest",current="newest",
        strongest="best",op="best",best="best",greatest="best",
        added="releas",add="releas",adding="releas",release="releas",released="releas",releases="releas",releasing="releas",
        launched="releas",launch="releas",came="releas",
        began="start",begin="start",started="start",start="start",begun="start",starting="start",
        like="love",likes="love",liked="love",loves="love",loved="love",enjoy="love",enjoys="love",enjoyed="love",
        hate="hat",hates="hat",hated="hat",hating="hat",dislike="hat",dislikes="hat",despise="hat",
        use="use",used="use",uses="use",using="use",
        kitty="cat",kitten="cat",kittens="cat",puppy="dog",doggo="dog",doggy="dog",pup="dog",
        colour="color",colours="color",favourite="favorite",favourites="favorite",
        cheapest="cheapest",lowest="lowest",
        dropped="releas",drop="releas",appeared="releas",appear="releas",introduced="releas",
    }
    do local extra={}
        for _,v in _pairs(CANON) do extra[v]=v end
        for k,v in _pairs(extra) do if CANON[k]==nil then CANON[k]=v end end
    end

    local PHR={
        {"brain%s+rot","brainrot","rot"},{"steal%s+a%s+brainrot","sab","steal"},{"stealabrainrot","sab","steal"},
        {"limited%s+time%s+mode","ltm","limited"},{"one%s+of%s+one","1of1","one"},{"(%d+)%s+of%s+(%d+)","%1of%2"," of "},
        {"tell%s+me"," ","tell"},{"give%s+me"," ","give"},{"show%s+me"," ","show"},{"gimme"," ","gimme"},
        {"let%s+me%s+know"," ","know"},{"which%s+one"," ","which"},{"this%s+one"," ","this"},{"that%s+one"," ","that"},
        {"strawberry%s+elephant","strawberryelephant","elephant"},{"spyder%s+elephant","spyderelephant","elephant"},
        {"spider%s+elephant","spyderelephant","elephant"},{"headless%s+horseman","headlesshorseman","horseman"},
        {"john%s+pork","johnpork","pork"},{"skibidi%s+toilet","skibiditoilet","toilet"},
        {"dragon%s+skeletonini","dragonskeletonini","skeletonini"},{"grow%s+a%s+garden","growagarden","garden"},
        {"grow%s+garden","growagarden","garden"},{"1%s+speed%s+keyboard%s+escape","1speedkeyboardescape","keyboard"},
        {"ye?a?rs%s+old","age","old"},
        {"c[oa]me?s?%s+out","releas","out"},{"coming%s+out","releas","out"},
        {"show%s+up","releas","show"},{"showed%s+up","releas","show"},
    }

    local function stem(w)
        local c=CANON[w]
        if c then return c end
        local n=#w
        if n<=3 then return w end
        if n>4 and ssub(w,-3)=="ies" then w=ssub(w,1,-4).."y"
        elseif ssub(w,-1)=="s" then
            local p=ssub(w,-2,-2)
            if p~="s" and p~="u" and p~="i" then w=ssub(w,1,-2) end
        end
        n=#w
        if n>5 and ssub(w,-3)=="ing" then w=ssub(w,1,-4)
        elseif n>4 and ssub(w,-2)=="ed" then w=ssub(w,1,-3) end
        c=CANON[w]
        if c then return c end
        if #w>3 and ssub(w,-1)=="e" then w=ssub(w,1,-2) end
        return w
    end

    local function tokenize(text)
        local x=S.basicClean(text)
        for i=1,#PHR do
            local p=PHR[i]
            if sfind(x,p[3],1,true) then x=sgsub(x,p[1],p[2]) end
        end
        if sfind(x,"brainrots",1,true) and (sfind(x,"remov",1,true) or sfind(x,"delet",1,true) or sfind(x,"rework",1,true)) then
            x=sgsub(x,"brainrots","brainrotsx")
        end
        x=S.applySynonyms(x)
        local set,list,cnt={}, {}, {}
        for w in sgmatch(x,"%w+") do
            if not STOP[w] then
                local cn=CARD[w]
                local t=cn and _tostring(cn) or stem(w)
                if t~=S_EMPTY and not STOP[t] then
                    local c=(cnt[t] or 0)+1
                    cnt[t]=c
                    if c>1 then t=t.."#"..c end
                    if not set[t] then set[t]=true list[#list+1]=t end
                end
            end
        end
        return list,set
    end
    S.tokenize=tokenize

    ------------------------------------------------------------ INDEX
    local IDX={ready=false,building=false}

    S.buildIndex=function(canYield)
        if IDX.ready then return true end
        if IDX.building then
            if canYield then return false end
            local t0=_oclock()
            while IDX.building and (_oclock()-t0)<8 do _wait(0.03) end
            return IDX.ready
        end
        IDX.building=true
        local ok,err=_pcall(function()
            local sigs,list,c={}, {},0
            for key,val in _pairs(DB) do
                local toks=tokenize(key)
                if #toks>0 then
                    tsort(toks)
                    local sig=tconcat(toks," ")
                    local ex=sigs[sig]
                    if ex then
                        if _tostring(val)~=_tostring(ex.val) then
                            local al=ex.alts
                            if not al then al={{key=ex.key,val=ex.val}} ex.alts=al end
                            al[#al+1]={key=key,val=val}
                        end
                        if #key<#ex.key or (#key==#ex.key and key<ex.key) then ex.key=key ex.val=val end
                    else
                        ex={key=key,val=val,toks=toks}
                        sigs[sig]=ex list[#list+1]=ex
                    end
                end
                c=c+1
                if canYield and c%150==0 then _wait() end
            end
            local post={}
            for i=1,#list do
                local e=list[i]
                for _,t in _ipairs(e.toks) do
                    local p=post[t]
                    if not p then p={} post[t]=p end
                    p[#p+1]=i
                end
            end
            local n=#list
            local idf,tokArr={}, {}
            for t,p in _pairs(post) do
                idf[t]=math.log((n+1)/(#p+0.5))+0.2
                tokArr[#tokArr+1]=t
            end
            for i=1,n do
                local e=list[i]
                local w=0
                for _,t in _ipairs(e.toks) do w=w+idf[t] end
                e.w=w
            end
            IDX.list,IDX.post,IDX.idf,IDX.tokArr,IDX.n=list,post,idf,tokArr,n
            IDX.common=n*0.12
            IDX.unkW=math.log((n+1)/1.5)+0.2
            IDX.fix={}
            IDX.ready=true
            S._IDX=IDX
        end)
        IDX.building=false
        return ok and IDX.ready
    end

    local function near(a,b)
        local la,lb=#a,#b
        if mabs(la-lb)>1 then return false end
        local i,j,diff=1,1,0
        while i<=la and j<=lb do
            if ssub(a,i,i)==ssub(b,j,j) then i=i+1 j=j+1
            else
                diff=diff+1
                if diff>1 then return false end
                if la==lb then
                    if ssub(a,i+1,i+1)==ssub(b,j,j) and ssub(a,i,i)==ssub(b,j+1,j+1) then i=i+2 j=j+2
                    else i=i+1 j=j+1 end
                elseif la>lb then i=i+1 else j=j+1 end
            end
        end
        diff=diff+(la-i+1)+(lb-j+1)
        return diff<=1
    end

    local function fixToken(t)
        if #t<4 then return nil end
        local f=IDX.fix[t]
        if f~=nil then return f or nil end
        local best,bestDf=nil,0
        for _,k in _ipairs(IDX.tokArr) do
            if #k>=4 and ssub(k,1,1)==ssub(t,1,1) and near(t,k) then
                local df=#IDX.post[k]
                if df>bestDf then best,bestDf=k,df end
            end
        end
        IDX.fix[t]=best or false
        return best
    end

    -- Keys that normalise to the same tokens but have different answers: pick the one worded closest to the question.
    local function pickAlt(e,text)
        local al=e.alts
        if not al then return e.val end
        local qw={}
        for w in sgmatch(S.applySynonyms(S.basicClean(text)),"%S+") do qw[w]=true end
        local bv,bs=e.val,-1
        for i=1,#al do
            local sc,n=0,0
            for w in sgmatch(S.applySynonyms(S.basicClean(al[i].key)),"%S+") do
                n=n+1
                if qw[w] then sc=sc+1 end
            end
            sc=sc-0.001*n
            if sc>bs then bs=sc bv=al[i].val end
        end
        return bv
    end

    -- Returns the DB value for the best fuzzy match, or nil when nothing is a confident match.
    S.fuzzyLookup=function(text)
        if not IDX.ready and not S.buildIndex(false) then return nil end
        local qlist=tokenize(text)
        if #qlist==0 then return nil end
        local idf,post,list,common,unkW=IDX.idf,IDX.post,IDX.list,IDX.common,IDX.unkW
        local qset,toks={}, {}
        local Wq,unk=0,0
        for _,t in _ipairs(qlist) do
            if not idf[t] then t=fixToken(t) or t end
            if not qset[t] then
                qset[t]=true toks[#toks+1]=t
                local w=idf[t]
                if w then Wq=Wq+w else Wq=Wq+unkW unk=unk+1 end
            end
        end
        local acc,anyRare={},false
        for _,t in _ipairs(toks) do
            local p=post[t]
            if p and #p<=common then
                anyRare=true
                for _,i in _ipairs(p) do acc[i]=true end
            end
        end
        if not anyRare then return nil end
        local best,bestScore,bestInter,bestW
        for i in _pairs(acc) do
            local e=list[i]
            local inter=0
            for _,t in _ipairs(e.toks) do if qset[t] then inter=inter+idf[t] end end
            local recall=inter/e.w
            if recall>=0.74 then
                local score=recall*(inter/Wq)
                if not best or score>bestScore+1e-9
                   or (mabs(score-bestScore)<=1e-9 and (inter>bestInter or (inter==bestInter and e.w<bestW))) then
                    best,bestScore,bestInter,bestW=e,score,inter,e.w
                end
            end
        end
        if best and bestScore>=0.5 then return pickAlt(best,text) end
        return nil
    end

    -- Reference lines for the AI prompt, built from the index (no full-DB rescans).
    S.indexRelevant=function(q,limit)
        if not IDX.ready and not S.buildIndex(false) then return S_EMPTY end
        local qlist=tokenize(q)
        if #qlist==0 then return S_EMPTY end
        local idf,post,list,common=IDX.idf,IDX.post,IDX.list,IDX.common
        local acc,qset={}, {}
        for _,t in _ipairs(qlist) do
            if not idf[t] then t=fixToken(t) or t end
            qset[t]=true
        end
        for t in _pairs(qset) do
            local p=post[t]
            if p and #p<=common then
                local w=idf[t]
                for _,i in _ipairs(p) do acc[i]=(acc[i] or 0)+w end
            end
        end
        local scored={}
        for i,a in _pairs(acc) do
            local e=list[i]
            scored[#scored+1]={e=e,s=a/(e.w^0.5)}
        end
        tsort(scored,function(x,y)
            if x.s~=y.s then return x.s>y.s end
            return x.e.key<y.e.key end)
        local out={}
        for i=1,_mmin(limit or 80,#scored) do
            out[#out+1]=scored[i].e.key.." = ".._tostring(scored[i].e.val)
        end
        return tconcat(out,"\n")
    end
end
-- ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

S._mixRules={["blue|red"]="PURPLE",["red|yellow"]="ORANGE",["blue|yellow"]="GREEN",["black|white"]="GRAY",
    ["red|white"]="PINK",["blue|white"]="LIGHTBLUE",["green|red"]="BROWN",["blue|green"]="TEAL",
    ["purple|red"]="MAGENTA",["cyan|magenta"]="BLUE",["green|yellow"]="LIME"}
S._allColors={red=1,blue=1,yellow=1,green=1,orange=1,purple=1,pink=1,brown=1,black=1,white=1,gray=1,grey=1,
    cyan=1,magenta=1,lime=1}
S.mixColors=function(a,b)if not a or not b then return nil end
    a,b=_slower(a),_slower(b)if not S._allColors[a]or not S._allColors[b]then return nil end
    local x,y=a,b if y<x then x,y=y,x end return S._mixRules[x.."|"..y]end
S.detectColorMix=function(q)local l=_slower(q)
    l=l:gsub("what color do you get when you mix"," "):gsub("what do you get when you mix"," ")
    l=l:gsub("what color do you get"," "):gsub("mix"," "):gsub("mixing"," "):gsub("combined"," ")
    l=l:gsub("combine"," "):gsub("plus"," "):gsub("and"," "):gsub("with"," ")
    local found={}for w in l:gmatch(PAT_ALPHA)do if S._allColors[w]and not found[w]then found[#found+1]=w end end
    if#found>=2 then return S.mixColors(found[1],found[2])end return nil end

S.MODIFIERS={lowest=1,highest=1,smallest=1,biggest=1,largest=1,greatest=1,minimum=1,maximum=1,min=1,max=1,
    first=1,last=1,newest=1,oldest=1,current=1,recent=1,most=1,op=1}
local function isMod(w)return S.MODIFIERS[w]==1 end

S.PROTECTED={["black and white"]="\1bw\1",["yin and yang"]="\1yy\1",["garama and madundung"]="\1ga\1",
    ["chipso and queso"]="\1cq\1",["pancake and syrup"]="\1ps\1",
    ["tung tung tung sahur and crocidilo penisini"]="\1ts\1",["tung tung tung sahur and crocodilo penisini"]="\1tc\1",
    ["sammyni spyderini and unclito samito and gattatino nyanino"]="\1sg\1"}
S.UNPROTECT={}for p,t in _pairs(S.PROTECTED)do S.UNPROTECT[t]=p end
S.DAY_NAMES={SUNDAY=1,MONDAY=1,TUESDAY=1,WEDNESDAY=1,THURSDAY=1,FRIDAY=1,SATURDAY=1}
S.DAY_CONTEXT={"day","days","date","when","birth","birthday","born","created","released","made","today","tomorrow",
    "yesterday","week","month","year","ago"}
S.answerIsDayName=function(c)return S.DAY_NAMES[c]==true end
S.questionAsksAboutDay=function(part)local ql=_slower(part or S_EMPTY)
    for _,w in _ipairs(S.DAY_CONTEXT)do if _sfind(ql,"%f[%a]"..w.."%f[%A]")then return true end end
    return false end
S.splitQuestions=function(text)local s=" ".._slower(_tostring(text or S_EMPTY)).." "
    for ph,tk in _pairs(S.PROTECTED)do s=s:gsub(ph,tk)end
    s=s:gsub("%s*[%?%;%,]%s*",S_AND):gsub("%s*&%s*",S_AND):gsub("%s*%+%s*",S_AND)
    s=" "..S.basicClean(s).." "
    s=s:gsub("%s+plus%s+",S_AND):gsub("%s+minus%s+",S_AND):gsub("%s+and%s+and%s+",S_AND)
    local parts={}
    for p in(s..S_AND):gmatch("(.-)%s+and%s+")do
        p=S.trim(p)if p~=S_EMPTY then for tk,ph in _pairs(S.UNPROTECT)do p=p:gsub(tk,ph)end parts[#parts+1]=p end end
    if#parts==0 then parts[1]=S.trim(S.basicClean(text))end
    return parts end

S.multiplierOf=function(text)local l=_slower(text)
    if _sfind(l,"twice",1,true)then return 2 end
    if _sfind(l,"thrice",1,true)then return 3 end
    local n=_tonumber(_smatch(l,"(%d+)%s*times"))if n and n>=2 and n<=10 then return n end
    for w,v in _pairs(S.CARDINALS)do if v>=2 and v<=10 and _sfind(l,"%f[%a]"..w.."%s+times")then return v end end
    return nil end
S.stripMultiplier=function(text)local s=text
    s=s:gsub("%s*%d+%s*times%s*"," "):gsub("%s*twice%s*"," "):gsub("%s*thrice%s*"," ")
    for w,v in _pairs(S.CARDINALS)do if v>=2 and v<=10 then s=s:gsub("%s*"..w.."%s+times%s*"," ")end end
    local r=S.trim(s)return r~=S_EMPTY and r or text end

S.resolvePart=function(part)
    part=S.mathReplace(part)
    local times=S.multiplierOf(part)
    local q=S.stripMultiplier(part)
    q=S.trim(q)if q==S_EMPTY then return nil end
    if q:match("^%-?%d+%.?%d*$")then return times and _srep(q,times)or q end
    local nq=S.stripFillers(S.applySynonyms(S.basicClean(q)))
    if nq:match("^%-?%d+%.?%d*$")and not S.SAB_DB[nq]then return times and _srep(nq,times)or nq end
    local mix=S.detectColorMix(q)if mix then return mix end
    local a=S.fastLookup(q)
    if not a then
        local q2=q:gsub("%f[%a]an%f[%A]"," "):gsub("%f[%a]a%f[%A]"," ")
        q2=S.trim(q2:gsub(PAT_WS," "))
        if q2~=q then a=S.fastLookup(q2)end end
    if not a then return nil end
    local clean=S.upperClean(a)
    if clean==S_EMPTY or clean=="AND"then return nil end
    if S.answerIsDayName(clean)and not S.questionAsksAboutDay(part)then return nil end
    return times and _srep(clean,times)or clean
end

S.solveRiddle=function(text)
    text=S.mathReplace(text)
    local lower=_slower(text)
    local hasJoin=lower:find("%f[%a]and%f[%A]")or lower:find("%f[%a]plus%f[%A]")
              or lower:find("%f[%a]minus%f[%A]")or _sfind(lower,",",1,true)
              or _sfind(lower,";",1,true)or _sfind(lower,"?",1,true)
              or _sfind(lower,"+",1,true)or _sfind(lower,"&",1,true)
    if not hasJoin then
        local r=S.resolvePart(text)if r then return r,nil,nil end
        return nil,nil,nil end
    local parts=S.splitQuestions(text)
    local slots={}local missing={}
    for i,p in _ipairs(parts)do
        local r=S.resolvePart(p)
        if r and r~=S_EMPTY then slots[i]=r
        else slots[i]=nil missing[#missing+1]={idx=i,text=p} end end
    if#missing==0 then
        local combined={}
        for i=1,#parts do if slots[i]then combined[#combined+1]=slots[i]end end
        local str=S.concat(combined,S_EMPTY)
        if str==S_EMPTY then return nil,nil,nil end
        return str,nil,nil end
    return nil,missing,slots
end

-- ═════════ FLEX FALLBACK: any-wording riddles + typo tolerance (used when the main solver has no answer) ═════════
;(function()
local lower,sub,find,gsub,match,gmatch,fmt,rep=string.lower,string.sub,string.find,string.gsub,string.match,string.gmatch,string.format,string.rep
local concat,sort=table.concat,table.sort
local floor,abs=math.floor,math.abs
-- ---------- canonical tokens (any wording -> same tokens) ----------
local STOP={}
for w in gmatch("a an the of in on at to is are was were be been am do does did you your yours can could would will shall should that those these it its has have had there what which whats please kindly say tell and or with by about as so then than also just very really kinda pretty most ok okay hows how out inside within into calculate compute solve equal equals result answer math","%S+") do STOP[w]=true end

local CM={}
local function grp(canon,list) for w in gmatch(list,"%S+") do CM[w]=canon end end

grp("color","color colour shade hue")

grp("rel","release released launch launched create created made publish published drop dropped introduce introduced add added come came appear appeared")
grp("date","when date")

grp("1st","first 1st oldest original earliest initial")

grp("7th","seventh 7th") grp("8th","eighth 8th") grp("9th","ninth 9th") grp("10th","tenth 10th")
grp("11th","eleventh 11th") grp("12th","twelfth 12th")
grp("0","zero") grp("1","one") grp("2","two") grp("3","three") grp("4","four") grp("5","five") grp("6","six")
grp("7","seven") grp("8","eight") grp("9","nine") grp("10","ten") grp("11","eleven") grp("12","twelve")

local CRIT={month=true,year=true,day=true,date=true,favorite=true,hate=true,color=true,many=true,
    best=true,worst=true,cheapest=true,today=true,rarest=true,hardest=true,expensive=true,new=true,next=true,rel=true}
local TEMP={month=true,year=true,day=true,date=true}

local function sing(w)
    local n=#w
    if n>3 and sub(w,-1)=="s" and sub(w,-2)~="ss" and sub(w,-2)~="us" and sub(w,-2)~="is" then return sub(w,1,n-1) end
    return w
end
local function isCrit(t) return CRIT[t] or find(t,"^%d+[strnd][tdh]$")~=nil end

local function canonTokens(text)
    local s=tostring(text or "")
    s=gsub(s,"\226\128\153","")
    s=gsub(s,"['`]","")
    s=S.basicClean(s)
    s=gsub(s,"[^%w%s]"," ")
    s=" "..s.." "
    s=gsub(s," brain rot "," brainrot ")
    s=gsub(s," tell me "," ") s=gsub(s," show me "," ") s=gsub(s," give me "," ") s=gsub(s," let me know "," ")
    s=gsub(s," steal a brainrot "," sab ")
    s=gsub(s," stealabrainrot "," sab ")
    local toks,seen={},{}
    for w in gmatch(s,"%S+") do
        local c
        if STOP[w] then c=false
        elseif CM[w]~=nil then c=CM[w]
        else
            local sg=sing(w)
            if STOP[sg] then c=false
            elseif CM[sg]~=nil then c=CM[sg]
            else c=sg end
        end
        if c and not seen[c] then seen[c]=true toks[#toks+1]=c end
    end
    return toks
end
S.canonTokens=canonTokens

local function critSig(toks)
    local c,hasTemp={},false
    for _,t in ipairs(toks) do
        if isCrit(t) then c[#c+1]=t if TEMP[t] then hasTemp=true end end
    end
    local hasPl,hasRm=false,false
    for _,t in ipairs(toks) do
        if t=="brainrots" then hasPl=true elseif t=="remove" or t=="rework" then hasRm=true end
    end
    if hasPl and hasRm then c[#c+1]="brainrots" end
    if hasTemp then
        local d={}
        for _,t in ipairs(c) do if t~="rel" then d[#d+1]=t end end
        c=d
    end
    sort(c)
    return concat(c,",")
end

-- ---------- fuzzy token equality (typos) ----------
local function lev(a,b,lim)
    local la,lb=#a,#b
    if abs(la-lb)>lim then return lim+1 end
    local prev={}
    for j=0,lb do prev[j]=j end
    for i=1,la do
        local cur={[0]=i}
        local best=i
        local ca=sub(a,i,i)
        for j=1,lb do
            local cost=(ca==sub(b,j,j)) and 0 or 1
            local v=prev[j]+1
            local x=cur[j-1]+1
            if x<v then v=x end
            x=prev[j-1]+cost
            if x<v then v=x end
            cur[j]=v
            if v<best then best=v end
        end
        if best>lim then return lim+1 end
        prev=cur
    end
    return prev[lb]
end
local eqMemo={}
local function teq(a,b)
    if a==b then return true end
    local la,lb=#a,#b
    if la<5 or lb<5 or abs(la-lb)>2 then return false end
    if sub(a,1,1)~=sub(b,1,1) then return false end
    if isCrit(a) or isCrit(b) or find(a,"%d") or find(b,"%d") then return false end
    local key=a.."|"..b
    local m=eqMemo[key]
    if m~=nil then return m end
    local lim=(la>=7 and lb>=7) and 2 or 1
    m=lev(a,b,lim)<=lim
    eqMemo[key]=m
    return m
end

-- ---------- index over the whole database (inverted index + vocabulary) ----------
local idx,canonMap,compMap,inv,vocab
local WT={my=0.4,name=0.7,brainrot=0.6,game=0.6,sab=0.3,get=0.5,need=0.5,rel=0.6,date=0.7,favorite=0.7}
local function compact(t) return (gsub(lower(tostring(t or "")),"[^%w]","")) end
local bst
local function build(yield)
    if idx then return end
    local DB=S.SAB_DB
    if not bst then
        local keys={}
        for k in pairs(DB) do keys[#keys+1]=k end
        sort(keys,function(a,b) local la,lb=#a,#b if la~=lb then return la<lb end return a<b end)
        bst={keys=keys,pos=1,I={},CMAP={},KMAP={},INV={},VOC={}}
    end
    local st=bst
    local keys,I,CMAP,KMAP,INV,VOC=st.keys,st.I,st.CMAP,st.KMAP,st.INV,st.VOC
    local nkeys,done=#keys,0
    while st.pos<=nkeys do
        local k=keys[st.pos]
        st.pos=st.pos+1
        local v=DB[k]
        local toks=canonTokens(k)
        local n=#toks
        if n>0 then
            local sorted,set={}, {}
            for i=1,n do local t=toks[i] sorted[i]=t set[t]=true end
            sort(sorted)
            local sk=concat(sorted," ")
            if CMAP[sk]==nil then CMAP[sk]=v end
            local ei=#I+1
            I[ei]={key=k,val=v,t=toks,set=set,n=n,crit=critSig(toks),G=set.get==true,N=set.need==true,W=(set.who or set.where) and true or false}
            for i=1,n do
                local t=toks[i]
                local l=INV[t]
                if not l then
                    l={} INV[t]=l
                    local c=sub(t,1,1)
                    local vb=VOC[c]
                    if not vb then vb={} VOC[c]=vb end
                    vb[#vb+1]=t
                end
                l[#l+1]=ei
            end
        end
        local ck=compact(k)
        if ck~="" and KMAP[ck]==nil then KMAP[ck]=v end
        if yield then
            done=done+1
            if done>=150 then
                done=0
                task.wait()
                if idx then return end
            end
        end
    end
    idx,canonMap,compMap,inv,vocab=I,CMAP,KMAP,INV,VOC
end
S.buildFlexIndex=function() build(false) end

-- fix typos: replace unknown words by the closest known word
local spMemo={}
local function spellAll(t)
    if inv[t] or #t<5 or isCrit(t) or find(t,"%d") then return {t} end
    local m=spMemo[t]
    if m then return m end
    local vb=vocab[sub(t,1,1)]
    local list={}
    if vb then
        local lt=#t
        for i=1,#vb do
            local w=vb[i]
            local lw=#w
            local d=lw-lt
            if d>=-2 and d<=2 and lw>=5 and not isCrit(w) and not find(w,"%d") then
                local lim=(lt>=7 and lw>=7) and 2 or 1
                local dd=lev(t,w,lim)
                if dd<=lim then list[#list+1]={w=w,d=dd,c=#inv[w]} end
            end
        end
    end
    sort(list,function(x,y) if x.d~=y.d then return x.d<y.d end if x.c~=y.c then return x.c>y.c end return x.w<y.w end)
    m={}
    for i=1,math.min(3,#list) do m[i]=list[i].w end
    if #m==0 then m[1]=t end
    spMemo[t]=m
    return m
end
local function spell(t) return spellAll(t)[1] end

local memo={}
local lookup
lookup=function(text,exactOnly)
    local raw=S.basicClean(text)
    if raw=="" then return nil end
    local mk=(exactOnly and "E|" or "F|")..raw
    local c=memo[mk]
    if c~=nil then return c[1],c[2],c[3] end
    local DB=S.SAB_DB
    local a,how,hk
    if DB[raw]~=nil then a,how,hk=DB[raw],"raw",raw end
    if a==nil then
        local sp=find(raw," ",1,true)
        if sp and not find(raw," ",sp+1,true) then
            local sw=sub(raw,sp+1).." "..sub(raw,1,sp-1)
            if DB[sw]~=nil then a,how,hk=DB[sw],"swap",raw end
        end
    end
    if a==nil then
        if not idx then build() end
        local qt=canonTokens(raw)
        local nq=#qt
        if nq>0 then
            local alts={}
            for i=1,nq do alts[i]=spellAll(qt[i]) qt[i]=alts[i][1] end
            local sorted={}
            for i=1,nq do sorted[i]=qt[i] end
            sort(sorted)
            local sk=concat(sorted," ")
            if canonMap[sk]~=nil then a,how,hk=canonMap[sk],"canon",sk
            else
                -- try the other close spellings (e.g. elefant -> elephant instead of elefanto)
                local tries=0
                local function walk(i,cur)
                    if a~=nil or tries>=24 then return end
                    if i>nq then
                        tries=tries+1
                        local srt={}
                        for x=1,nq do srt[x]=cur[x] end
                        sort(srt)
                        local k2=concat(srt," ")
                        if canonMap[k2]~=nil then a,how,hk=canonMap[k2],"canon",k2 end
                        return
                    end
                    for _,w in ipairs(alts[i]) do cur[i]=w walk(i+1,cur) if a~=nil then return end end
                end
                walk(1,{})
            end
            if a==nil then
                local ck=compact(raw)
                if compMap[ck]~=nil then a,how,hk=compMap[ck],"compact",ck end
            end
            if a==nil and not exactOnly then
                local qset={}
                for i=1,nq do qset[qt[i]]=true end
                nq=0 local uq={}
                for t in pairs(qset) do nq=nq+1 uq[nq]=t end
                local qcrit=critSig(uq)
                local qG,qN=qset.get==true,qset.need==true
                local qW=(qset.who or qset.where) and true or false
                local cnt={}
                for i=1,nq do
                    local l=inv[uq[i]]
                    if l then for j=1,#l do local ei=l[j] cnt[ei]=(cnt[ei] or 0)+1 end end
                end
                local best,bestScore,bestLen
                for ei,m in pairs(cnt) do
                    local e=idx[ei]
                    if e.crit==qcrit and not (qG and e.N) and not (qN and e.G) and not (e.W and not qW) then
                        local uK,uQ=e.n-m,nq-m
                        local ok=false
                        if uK==0 then
                            if e.n>=2 then ok=(uQ<=3) else ok=(uQ==0) end
                        elseif e.n>=4 and uK==1 and uQ<=1 then ok=true end
                        if ok then
                            local mw,uQw,uKw=0,0,0
                            for i=1,nq do
                                local t=uq[i]
                                if e.set[t] then mw=mw+(WT[t] or 1) else uQw=uQw+(WT[t] or 1) end
                            end
                            for i=1,e.n do
                                local t=e.t[i]
                                if not qset[t] then uKw=uKw+(WT[t] or 1) end
                            end
                            local score=mw*10-uQw*3-uKw*8
                            if not best or score>bestScore or (score==bestScore and #e.key<bestLen) then
                                best,bestScore,bestLen=e,score,#e.key
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                if best then a,how,hk=best.val,"fuzzy",best.key end
            end
        end
    end
    memo[mk]={a,how,hk}
    return a,how,hk
end
S.flexLookup=lookup

-- cheap, cached "closest facts" for the AI prompt (replaces the slow full-database scan)
S.relevantDbEntries=function(q,limit)
    limit=limit or 80
    if not idx then build() end
    local qt=canonTokens(q)
    if #qt==0 then return "" end
    local cnt={}
    for i=1,#qt do
        local t=spell(qt[i])
        local l=inv[t]
        if l then for j=1,#l do local ei=l[j] cnt[ei]=(cnt[ei] or 0)+1 end end
    end
    local list={}
    for ei,m in pairs(cnt) do list[#list+1]={ei=ei,m=m} end
    sort(list,function(x,y)
        if x.m~=y.m then return x.m>y.m end
        local kx,ky=idx[x.ei].key,idx[y.ei].key
        if #kx~=#ky then return #kx<#ky end
        return kx<ky
    end)
    local out={}
    for i=1,math.min(limit,#list) do
        local e=idx[list[i].ei]
        out[#out+1]=e.key.." = "..tostring(e.val)
    end
    return concat(out,"\n")
end

local function flexResolve(part)
    part=tostring(part or "")
    if S.mathReplace then part=S.mathReplace(part) end
    local times=S.multiplierOf(part)
    local q=S.trim(S.stripMultiplier(part))
    if q=="" then return nil end
    -- "how much is 26" / "what is 26" -> 26
    local qt=canonTokens(q)
    local digit,other=nil,0
    for _,t in ipairs(qt) do
        if match(t,"^%-?%d+%.?%d*$") then if digit then other=other+1 else digit=t end
        elseif t~="many" then other=other+1 end
    end
    if digit and other==0 then return times and rep(digit,times) or digit end
    local a=lookup(q)
    if a==nil then return nil end
    local clean=S.upperClean(a)
    if clean=="" or clean=="AND" then return nil end
    if S.answerIsDayName(clean) and not S.questionAsksAboutDay(part) then return nil end
    return times and rep(clean,times) or clean
end

local MIXW={"mix","mixed","mixing","combine","combined","combining","blend","blended","merge","together"}
local function mixWhole(text)
    local l=lower(tostring(text or ""))
    local has=find(l,"get when",1,true)~=nil
    for _,w in ipairs(MIXW) do if find(l,"%f[%a]"..w.."%f[%A]") then has=true end end
    if not has then return nil end
    local found,seen={}, {}
    for w in gmatch(l,"%a+") do
        if S._allColors[w] and not seen[w] then seen[w]=true found[#found+1]=w end
    end
    if #found>=2 then return S.mixColors(found[1],found[2]) end
    return nil
end

local baseResolve=S.resolvePart
S.resolvePart=function(part,exactOnly)
    local r=baseResolve(part,exactOnly)
    if r then return r end
    local ok,res=pcall(flexResolve,part)
    if ok and res then return res end
    return nil
end

local baseSolve=S.solveRiddle
S.solveRiddle=function(text)
    local ok,mix=pcall(mixWhole,text)
    if ok and mix then return mix,nil,nil end
    return baseSolve(text)
end

-- answers every question in the order it was asked: returns { {text=..., ans=... or nil}, ... }
S.solveOrdered=function(text)
    local t=tostring(text or "")
    if S.mathReplace then t=S.mathReplace(t) end
    local ok,mix=pcall(mixWhole,t)
    if ok and mix then return {{text=t,ans=mix}} end
    local l=lower(t)
    local hasJoin=find(l,"%f[%a]and%f[%A]") or find(l,"%f[%a]plus%f[%A]") or find(l,"%f[%a]minus%f[%A]")
        or find(l,",",1,true) or find(l,";",1,true) or find(l,"?",1,true) or find(l,"&",1,true) or find(l,"+",1,true)
    if not hasJoin then
        return {{text=t,ans=S.resolvePart(t)}}
    end
    local okw,whole=pcall(lookup,t,true)
    if okw and whole~=nil then
        local clean=S.upperClean(whole)
        if clean~="" then return {{text=t,ans=clean}} end
    end
    local out={}
    for i,p in ipairs(S.splitQuestions(t)) do
        local okp,ans=pcall(S.resolvePart,p)
        out[i]={text=p,ans=(okp and ans~="" and ans) or nil}
    end
    if #out==0 then out[1]={text=t,ans=nil} end
    return out
end

if task and task.spawn then task.spawn(function() if task.wait then task.wait(0.5) end pcall(build,true) end) end
end)()

do local function isFn(v)return _type(v)=="function"end
   local function collect()
       local list,seen={}, {}
       local function push(fn,lbl)if not isFn(fn)or seen[fn]then return end seen[fn]=true list[#list+1]={fn=fn,label=lbl}end
       for _,n in _ipairs({"request","http_request","httprequest","HttpGet","HttpPost"})do
           push(_rawget(_G,n),n)end
       local ok,genv=_pcall(getgenv)if ok and _type(genv)=="table"then
           for _,n in _ipairs({"request","http_request","httprequest"})do push(genv[n],"getgenv()."..n)end end
       return list end
   local function norm(r)if _type(r)=="string"then return{StatusCode=200,Body=r}end
       if _type(r)=="table"then
           local s=_tonumber(r.StatusCode or r.Status or r.code or 200)
           local b=r.Body or r.body or r.Data or r.data or r.Response
           if b==nil then b=r[1]end
           if b~=nil then return{StatusCode=s,Body=_tostring(b)}end end return nil end
   local winner=nil
   S.fullRequest=function(opts)
       if winner then local ok,res=_pcall(winner.fn,opts)
           if ok then local n=norm(res)if n then return n end end winner=nil end
       local c=collect()if#c==0 then return nil,"no http"end
       local lastErr="all failed"
       for _,x in _ipairs(c)do
           local ok,res=_pcall(x.fn,opts)
           if ok then local n=norm(res)if n then winner=x return n end lastErr=x.label..": bad"
           else lastErr=x.label..": ".._tostring(res)end end
       return nil,lastErr end end

   S._HH_Conns={}S.isDead=false
   local k=_INew("BindableEvent")k.Name=sn k.Parent=S.safeParent
   k.Event:Connect(function()S.isDead=true
       for _,c in _ipairs(S._HH_Conns)do _pcall(function()c:Disconnect()end)end S._HH_Conns={}end)
   S.trackConn=function(c)if S.isDead then return c end _tinsert(S._HH_Conns,c)return c end
   S.isAlive=function()return not S.isDead end end

-- AI rate limiter (from v43.2)
do local W=1200 M=30 H={}
   local function prune()local now=_tick()local cut=now-W local k={}
       for i=1,#H do if H[i]>=cut then k[#k+1]=H[i]end end H=k end
   S.canMakeAiRequest=function()prune()
       if#H>=M then return false,_sformat("limit (%d/%d in 20min)",#H,M)end return true end
   S.recordAiRequest=function()H[#H+1]=_tick()end end

                           {m="openai/gpt-oss-120b",effort="low",   tok=360,to=4.5,delay=1.6}}},

                           {m="openai/gpt-oss-20b", effort="medium",tok=700,to=5.0,delay=3.0}}},
       auto ={dl=6.0,grace=0.8,list={{m="openai/gpt-oss-120b",effort="medium",tok=700,to=5.5,delay=0},

   S.getAiModels=function()
