       local ok,res=_pcall(S.indexRelevant,q,limit)
       if ok and _type(res)=="string"then return res end
       return S_EMPTY end
   S.SYSTEM_PROMPT=[[You are the answer engine inside a "Steal a Brainrot" (Roblox) code-sniping tool. A server announcer asks a riddle and the player types the answer as a CODE. Your one line is typed straight into the game, so it must be exact and fast.

SOURCES, in priority order
1. REFERENCE block in the user message: lines "question = ANSWER" written by the owner. They are ground truth. If a line means the same thing as the question (even worded differently), output its ANSWER exactly.
2. LIVE CONTEXT in the user message (latest update, newest brainrot, current date and time). Use it for "today", "newest", "latest", "this update", "what day/month/year/time is it".
3. PLAYER FACTS below (the owner, "Sammy"). Use them for me / my / I / Sammy questions.
4. Your own knowledge, only for general trivia and arithmetic.
If none of these answers it, output UNKNOWN. Never invent a game fact that is not in the reference.

PLAYER FACTS: name SAMMY, 2nd/middle/last name BRAKTA, cat NOVA, dog ROCKY, favorite number 67 (he adds 67 to codes), favorite game FORTNITE, hated game GROWAGARDEN, competitive game VALORANT, first game played CRABGAME, hated studio SECRETVERSE, hated person SECRETLOKII, enemy/admin-abuse host JANDEL, youtuber friend with food in his name STEAK, hair BLACK, roblox avatar and car RED, skin YELLOW, favorite color BLUE, favorite food TACO, favorite brainrot MEOWL, favorite mutation GALAXY, favorite artist DRAKE, phone IPHONE, device PC, country USA, season now AUTUMN, birthday FEBRUARY24 (born 2002, age 24), started making Roblox games in 2013, weight 250.

OUTPUT FORMAT
- Exactly one line: "CODE: <ANSWER>" for text or "NUMBER: <digits>" for a pure number.
- UPPERCASE, no spaces, no punctuation, no quotes, no explanation, nothing else.

RULES
- Multi-part riddle (separate questions joined by "and", ",", "&"): answer EVERY part in the order asked and join them with NO spaces. "my cat name and 7 times 3" -> NOVA21. A TARGET QUESTION may be one part of a FULL RIDDLE: answer only the target.
- Arithmetic: compute exactly (plus, minus, times, divided by, number words). "7 times 3" is math (21), not repetition. "cat plus dog" is two separate questions, not math.
- "twice" / "thrice" / "N times" after a NON-math thing repeats that answer: "my favorite number twice" -> 6767.
- Dates: month only -> OCTOBER; year only -> 2025; day number only -> 1; full date -> OCTOBER2025.
- Brainrot names are joined with no spaces: STRAWBERRYELEPHANT, DRAGONSKELETONINI. "skeleton", "skellington" and "skeltonini" all mean DRAGONSKELETONINI.
- Colors mixed: blue+red=PURPLE, red+yellow=ORANGE, blue+yellow=GREEN, black+white=GRAY, red+white=PINK, blue+white=LIGHTBLUE.
- "biggest/largest animal or whale" -> BLUEWHALE.
- "how many ..." counts come from the reference.
- Understand typos, slang and any word order ("strawbery elefant colour", "whats the doggo called").
- Resolve chains ("opposite of opposite of hot" -> HOT).
- Prefer the reference over your own memory whenever both could apply.

EXAMPLES
what month did meowl come out -> CODE: OCTOBER
seven times three and my name -> CODE: 21SAMMY
mix blue and red -> CODE: PURPLE
how many one of one brainrots exist -> NUMBER: 155
my favorite number twice -> NUMBER: 6767
colour of the first og and my cat name -> CODE: REDNOVA]]
   local AUTH_HEADERS={["Authorization"]="Bearer "..S.RIDDLE_API_KEY,
       ["Content-Type"]="application/json",["Accept"]="application/json"}
   local aiCache={}
   local function callAI(e,uc)
       local function send(extras)
           local req={model=e.m,
               messages={{role="system",content=S.SYSTEM_PROMPT},{role="user",content=uc}},
               temperature=0,max_completion_tokens=e.tok,stream=false}
           if extras then req.reasoning_effort=e.effort req.include_reasoning=false end
           local resp,err=S.fullRequest({Url=S.RIDDLE_API_URL,Method="POST",
               Headers=AUTH_HEADERS,Body=S.HttpService:JSONEncode(req),Timeout=e.to})
           if not resp then return nil,nil,err end
           return resp.StatusCode,resp.Body,nil end
       local st,rb,err=send(not S._aiNoExtras)
       if st==400 and not S._aiNoExtras then
           S._aiNoExtras=true
           st,rb,err=send(false) end
       return st,rb,err end
   local function isArithmetic(q)local l=_slower(q)
       return _sfind(l,"%f[%a]plus%f[%A]")~=nil or _sfind(l,"%f[%a]minus%f[%A]")~=nil
           or _sfind(l,"%f[%a]times%f[%A]")~=nil or _sfind(l,"+",1,true)~=nil or _sfind(l,"*",1,true)~=nil end
   local function parse(content,question)
       if not content or content==S_EMPTY then return nil end
       content=content:gsub("<thinking>[%s%S]-</thinking>","")
       content=content:gsub("<think>[%s%S]-</think>","")
       content=content:gsub("<reasoning>[%s%S]-</reasoning>","")
       content=content:gsub("```[%s%S]-```","")
       local function strip(s)s=_tostring(s or S_EMPTY)
           s=s:gsub("[Cc][Oo][Dd][Ee]:%s*",S_EMPTY)
           s=s:gsub("[Aa][Nn][Ss][Ww][Ee][Rr]:%s*",S_EMPTY)
           return S.trim(s)end
       local ans,alt
       local n=_smatch(content,"[Nn][Uu][Mm][Bb][Ee][Rr]:%s*([%-%d%.]+)")
       if n then ans=n end
       if not ans then
           for line in content:gmatch("[^\r\n]+")do
               line=S.trim(line)
               if line:match("^[Cc][Oo][Dd][Ee]:")then
                   local rest=_smatch(line,"^%a+:%s*(.*)$")or S_EMPTY
                   ans=S.upperClean(strip(rest))break end end end
       if not ans then
           local last=nil
           for line in content:gmatch("[^\r\n]+")do
               line=S.trim(line)
               if line~=S_EMPTY and not line:match("^<")and not line:match("^%[")then last=line end end
           if last then ans=S.upperClean(strip(last))end end
       if not ans or ans==S_EMPTY then return nil end
       local up=_supper(S.trim(ans))
       if up==S_UNKNOWN or up==S_NA or up==S_NONE then return nil end
       if up:find(S_UNKNOWN,1,true) or up:find(S_NA,1,true)then return nil end
       if ans:match("^%-?%d+%.?%d*$")then
           local num=_tonumber(ans)
           if not num then return nil end
           if not isArithmetic(question)and#ans<=1 then return nil end end
       if#ans>=6 then
           local ql=_slower(_tostring(question))
           local wantsRepeat=_sfind(ql,"twice",1,true)or _sfind(ql,"thrice",1,true)or _sfind(ql,"times",1,true)
           if not wantsRepeat then
               local h=_mfloor(#ans/2)
               if _ssub(ans,1,h)==_ssub(ans,h+1,h*2)then return nil end end end
       if not ans:match("^%-?%d+%.?%d*$")and#ans>1 then ans=S.upperClean(ans)end
       return ans,alt
   end
   local function buildUser(q,ctx)
       local t=_odate("*t")
       local L={}
       L[#L+1]="TARGET QUESTION: ".._tostring(q)
       if ctx and ctx~=q then
           L[#L+1]="FULL RIDDLE (context only, answer ONLY the target question): ".._tostring(ctx) end
       L[#L+1]=""
       L[#L+1]="LIVE CONTEXT"
       L[#L+1]="PLAYER PLATFORM: PC / LAPTOP"
       L[#L+1]="LATEST UPDATE NUMBER: "..S.getLatestUpdateNumber()
       L[#L+1]="LATEST UPDATE DATE: "..S.getLatestUpdateDate()
       local newest=S.getMostRecentBrainrot()
       if newest~=S_EMPTY then L[#L+1]="NEWEST BRAINROT FROM LATEST UPDATE: "..newest end
       L[#L+1]="CURRENT DATE: "..S.getDateLong()
       L[#L+1]="CURRENT DAY: "..S.getDayName()
       L[#L+1]="CURRENT MONTH: "..S.getMonthName()
       L[#L+1]="CURRENT YEAR: ".._tostring(t.year)
       L[#L+1]="CURRENT TIME (12H): "..S.getTime12Short()
       local dbRef=S.relevantDbEntries(ctx and ctx~=q and (_tostring(q).." "..ctx) or q,70)
       if dbRef~=S_EMPTY then L[#L+1]=""L[#L+1]="REFERENCE:" L[#L+1]=dbRef end
       return S.concat(L,"\n") end
   local function queryModel(e,uc,q)
       local st,rb=callAI(e,uc)
       if st and st>=200 and st<300 and _type(rb)=="string"then
           local ok2,dec=_pcall(function()return S.HttpService:JSONDecode(rb)end)
           if ok2 and _type(dec)=="table"then
               local ch=dec.choices and dec.choices[1]
               local c=ch and ch.message and ch.message.content
               if c and c~=S_EMPTY then
                   local a=parse(c,q)
                   if a then return a end end end end
       return nil end

   S.solveWithApi=function(q,ctx)
       if not S.cfg.useAI then return nil end
       local key=_slower(_tostring(q))
       local hit=aiCache[key]
       if hit then return hit end
       local ok,why=S.canMakeAiRequest()
       if not ok then
           if S.logStatus then S.logStatus("AI skipped: "..why,S.T.Orange)end
           return nil end
       local mode=S.AI_MODES[S.cfg.aiMode or "fast"] or S.AI_MODES.fast
       local uc=buildUser(q,ctx)
       local t0=S.nowSec()
       local deadline=t0+mode.dl
       local list=mode.list
       local n=#list
       local res,fin,failedFirst,answer={},0,false,nil
       for i=1,n do
           local e=list[i]
           _spawn(function()
               if i>1 and e.delay>0 then
                   while not answer and not failedFirst and S.nowSec()<t0+e.delay do _wait(0.03)end end
               if not answer then
                   local okq,a=_pcall(queryModel,e,uc,q)
                   res[i]=(okq and a) or false
                   if not res[i] and i==1 then failedFirst=true end end
               fin=fin+1 end)
       end
       local graceStart=nil
       while not answer and S.nowSec()<deadline do
           if mode.grace then
               local sm,fa=res[1],res[2]
               if sm then answer=sm
               elseif fa then
                   graceStart=graceStart or S.nowSec()
                   if res[1]==false or (S.nowSec()-graceStart)>=mode.grace then answer=fa end end
           else
               for i=1,n do if res[i] then answer=res[i] break end end end
           if answer or fin>=n then break end
           _wait(0.025)
       end
       if not answer then
           for i=1,n do if res[i] then answer=res[i] break end end end
       if answer then
           S.recordAiRequest()
           aiCache[key]=answer
           return answer end
       return nil end
end

do local BS=400 DS=16 mode=nil conns={}cd={}boost=false
   local function cache()local c=S.player.Character if not c then return false end
       local h=c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")local r=c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
       if not h or not r then return false end cd={character=c,humanoid=h,root=r}return true end
   local function discon()for _,c in _ipairs(conns)do _pcall(function()c:Disconnect()end)end conns={}end
   local function isRag()if not cd.humanoid then return false end
       local s=cd.humanoid:GetState()
       if s==_EHS.Physics or s==_EHS.Ragdoll or s==_EHS.FallingDown then return true end
       local t=S.player:GetAttribute("RagdollEndTime")
       if t and(t-_workspace:GetServerTimeNow())>0 then return true end return false end
   local function exitRag()if not cd.humanoid or not cd.root then return end
       _pcall(function()S.player:SetAttribute("RagdollEndTime",_workspace:GetServerTimeNow())end)
       for _,d in _ipairs(cd.character:GetDescendants())do
           if d:IsA("BallSocketConstraint")or(d:IsA("Attachment")and d.Name:find("RagdollAttachment"))then d:Destroy()end end
       if not boost then boost=true cd.humanoid.WalkSpeed=BS end
       if cd.humanoid.Health>0 then cd.humanoid:ChangeState(_EHS.Running)end
       cd.root.Anchored=false end
   local function loop()while mode=="v1"do _wait()
       local r=isRag()if r then exitRag()
       elseif boost and not r then boost=false if cd.humanoid then cd.humanoid.WalkSpeed=DS end end end end
   S.startAntiRagdoll=function()if mode=="v1"then return end
       if not cache()then return end mode="v1"
       local c=S.RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
           local cam=_workspace.CurrentCamera
           if cam and cd.humanoid then cam.CameraSubject=cd.humanoid end end)
       _tinsert(conns,c)
       local r=S.player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()boost=false _wait(0.5)cache()end)
       _tinsert(conns,r)_spawn(loop)end
   S.stopAntiRagdoll=function()mode=nil
       if boost and cd.humanoid then cd.humanoid.WalkSpeed=DS end
       boost=false discon()cd={}end end

do local L=RS("Lighting")W=_workspace
   S._antiLagConns={}S._antiLagActive=false S._antiLagOriginal=nil
   local function procI(v)
       if v:IsA("ParticleEmitter")or v:IsA("Trail")or v:IsA("Smoke")or v:IsA("Fire")then
           _pcall(function()v.Enabled=false end)
       elseif v:IsA("BasePart")then _pcall(function()
           if v.Material~=_EMat.Plastic then v.Material=_EMat.Plastic end
           if v.CastShadow then v.CastShadow=false end end)end end
   local function procL(v)
       if v:IsA("BloomEffect")or v:IsA("BlurEffect")or v:IsA("SunRaysEffect")then
           _pcall(function()v.Enabled=false end)end end
   S.startAntiLag=function()if S._antiLagActive then return end
       S._antiLagActive=true S.cfg.antiLag=true S.saveConfig()
       _pcall(function()L.GlobalShadows=false L.FogEnd=1e9 L.Brightness=1 L.ClockTime=12 end)
       for _,v in _ipairs(L:GetChildren())do procL(v)end
       for _,v in _ipairs(W:GetDescendants())do procI(v)end
       _tinsert(S._antiLagConns,W.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(v)
           if not S._antiLagActive then return end _defer(procI,v)end))end
   S.stopAntiLag=function()if not S._antiLagActive then return end
       S._antiLagActive=false S.cfg.antiLag=false S.saveConfig()
       for _,c in _ipairs(S._antiLagConns)do _pcall(function()c:Disconnect()end)end S._antiLagConns={}
   end end

do local function pv(t)t=_slower(t or S_EMPTY)local n=_tonumber(_smatch(t,PAT_CURR_NUM))or 0
       if _sfind(t,"k",1,true)then n=n*1e3 elseif _sfind(t,"m",1,true)then n=n*1e6
       elseif _sfind(t,"b",1,true)then n=n*1e9 elseif _sfind(t,"t",1,true)then n=n*1e12 end return n end
   S.labels=function(part)local dn,gen
       if not part or not part.Parent then return nil,nil end
       for _,c in _ipairs(part:GetChildren())do
           if c:IsA("BillboardGui")or c:IsA("SurfaceGui")then
               for _,l in _ipairs(c:GetDescendants())do if l:IsA("TextLabel")then
                   if l.Name=="DisplayName"then dn=l elseif l.Name=="Generation"then gen=l end end end
               if dn or gen then return dn,gen end end end
       return dn,gen end
   S.scanCarpetBrainrots=function()
       if not S.isRealSAB() then return {} end
       local out={}local rp=S.player.Character and S.player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
       local seen={}
       local function consider(part,owner,attached)
           if not part or not part.Parent then return end
           if seen[part]then return end seen[part]=true
           local dn,gen=S.labels(part)
           if not dn or not gen then return end
           local nameText=_tostring(dn.Text or S_EMPTY)
           if nameText==S_EMPTY or#nameText>64 then return end
           local dist=rp and(part.Position-rp.Position).Magnitude or 0
           _tinsert(out,{part=part,displayName=nameText,value=pv(gen.Text),position=part.Position,
               velocity=part.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude,distance=dist,carried=attached,owner=owner})end
       for _,pl in _ipairs(S.Players:GetPlayers())do
           local c=pl.Character if c then for _,o in _ipairs(c:GetDescendants())do
               if o:IsA("BasePart")then consider(o,pl.Name,pl~=S.player)end end end end
       local d=_workspace:FindFirstChild("Debris")
       if d then for _,o in _ipairs(d:GetChildren())do
           if o:IsA("BasePart")then consider(o,nil,false)
           elseif o:IsA("Model")then for _,sub in _ipairs(o:GetDescendants())do
               if sub:IsA("BasePart")then consider(sub,nil,false)end end end end end
       _tsort(out,function(a,b)return a.value>b.value end)return out end
   local chase={active=false,target=nil,conn=nil,speed=59,lastPos=nil,goneSince=nil}
   S.stopChase=function(reason)
       if chase.conn then _pcall(function()chase.conn:Disconnect()end)chase.conn=nil end
       chase.active=false chase.target=nil
       local c=S.player.Character local hrp=c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
       if hrp then hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity=_V3zero hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity=_V3zero end
       if S.setStatus then S.setStatus(reason or "Chase stopped.",S.T.TextGray)end end
   S.chaseTo=function(part,speed)
       S.stopChase()
       if not part or not part.Parent then return end
       chase.active=true chase.target=part chase.speed=speed or 59 chase.lastPos=part.Position
       if S.setStatus then S.setStatus("Locked on.",S.T.Green)end
       chase.conn=S.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
           if not chase.active then return end
           local tp=chase.target
           if not tp then S.stopChase("Target lost.")return end
           if not tp.Parent then chase.goneSince=chase.goneSince or S.nowSec()
               if(S.nowSec()-chase.goneSince)>30 then S.stopChase("Target gone.")return end
           else chase.goneSince=nil chase.lastPos=tp.Position end
           local c=S.player.Character
           local hrp=c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
           local hum=c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
           if not hrp or not hum or hum.Health<=0 then return end
           local aimPos=(tp.Parent and tp.Position)or chase.lastPos
           if not aimPos then return end
           local diff=aimPos-hrp.Position local dist=diff.Magnitude
           local dir=diff.Magnitude>0.01 and diff.Unit or _V3zero
           local spd=chase.speed if dist<4 then spd=_mmax(chase.speed*0.35,25)end
           hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity=dir*spd hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity=_V3zero end)end
   S.isChasing=function()return chase.active end
   S.checkAutoChase=function()
       if not S.isRealSAB() then return end
       if not S.cfg.autoChase then return end
       if S.isChasing() then return end
       local minVal=_tonumber(S.cfg.chaseMinValue)or 25e6
       local found
       local ok=_pcall(function()found=S.scanCarpetBrainrots()end)
       if not ok or not found or #found==0 then return end
       for _,pet in _ipairs(found)do
           if not pet.carried and not pet.owner and pet.value>=minVal then
               S.chaseTo(pet.part)
               return end end end
   _spawn(function()
       while S.isAlive()do _wait(0.25)_pcall(S.checkAutoChase)end end)
end

do
   local charConn=nil local anchorLoop=nil
   local function applyAnchor(c)
       if not c then return end
       for _, p in _ipairs(c:GetDescendants()) do
           if p:IsA("BasePart") then _pcall(function() p.Anchored = true end) end end
   end
   local function clearAnchor(c)
       if not c then return end
       for _, p in _ipairs(c:GetDescendants()) do
           if p:IsA("BasePart") then _pcall(function() p.Anchored = false end) end end
       local hum = c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
       if hum then _pcall(function() hum.PlatformStand = false hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running) end) end
   end
   local function startAnchorLoop()
       if anchorLoop then return end
       anchorLoop = _spawn(function()
           while S.cfg.anchor and S.isAlive() do
               _wait(0.15)
               local c = S.player.Character
               if c then for _, p in _ipairs(c:GetDescendants()) do
                   if p:IsA("BasePart") and not p.Anchored then
                       _pcall(function() p.Anchored = true end) end end end
           end
           anchorLoop = nil
       end)
   end
   S.updateAnchor = function()
       if S.cfg.anchor then
           if S.player.Character then applyAnchor(S.player.Character) end
           if not charConn then
               charConn = S.player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(c)
                   _defer(function() c:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart",5) if S.cfg.anchor then applyAnchor(c) end end)
               end)
           end
           startAnchorLoop()
       else
           if S.player.Character then clearAnchor(S.player.Character) end
           if charConn then _pcall(function() charConn:Disconnect() end) charConn = nil end
           if anchorLoop then _pcall(_cancel, anchorLoop) anchorLoop = nil end
       end
   end
end

do
   S._autoBuyConns = {} S._autoBuyActive = false S._autoBuyLoop = nil S._autoBuyIndex = {}
   local BUY_WORDS = {"purchase", "buy", "confirm purchase"}
   local function matchesBuy(t)
       if not t or t == S_EMPTY then return false end
       local l = _slower(t)
       for _, w in _ipairs(BUY_WORDS) do if _sfind(l, w, 1, true) then return true end end
       return false end
   local function addToIndex(p)
       if not p or not p.Parent or not p:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then return end
       local at = p.ActionText or S_EMPTY local ot = p.ObjectText or S_EMPTY
       if matchesBuy(at) or matchesBuy(ot) then
           S._autoBuyIndex[p] = true
           _pcall(function() p.HoldDuration=0 p.MaxActivationDistance=250 p.RequiresLineOfSight=false end)
       end end
   local function rebuildIndex()
       S._autoBuyIndex = {}
       for _, obj in _ipairs(_workspace:GetDescendants()) do
           if obj:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then addToIndex(obj) end end end
   local function firePrompt(p)
       if not p or not p.Parent then return end
       _pcall(function()
           if p.HoldDuration ~= 0 then p.HoldDuration = 0 end
           if p.MaxActivationDistance < 250 then p.MaxActivationDistance = 250 end
           if p.RequiresLineOfSight then p.RequiresLineOfSight = false end
           if not p.Enabled then p.Enabled = true end end)
       if S.fsignal then
           _pcall(function() S.fsignal(p.PromptButtonHoldBegan) end)
           _pcall(function() S.fsignal(p.Triggered, S.player) end) end
       if S.gconns then
           _pcall(function()
               local cs = S.gconns(p.PromptButtonHoldBegan)
               if _type(cs) == "table" then for _, c in _ipairs(cs) do
                   _pcall(function() if c.Enabled ~= false then c:Fire() end end) end end end)
       end end
   local function fireAllIndexed()
       for p in _pairs(S._autoBuyIndex) do if p.Parent then firePrompt(p) end end end
   S.startAutoBuy = function()
       if S._autoBuyActive then return end
       S._autoBuyActive = true S.cfg.autoBuy = true S.saveConfig()
       rebuildIndex()
       _tinsert(S._autoBuyConns, _workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
           if not S._autoBuyActive then return end
           if obj:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then _defer(addToIndex, obj) end end))
       if not S._autoBuyLoop then
           S._autoBuyLoop = _spawn(function()
               local lastRebuild = S.nowSec()
               while S._autoBuyActive and S.isAlive() do
                   _pcall(fireAllIndexed)
                   if (S.nowSec() - lastRebuild) > 5.0 then _pcall(rebuildIndex) lastRebuild = S.nowSec() end
                   _wait(0.15) end
               S._autoBuyLoop = nil end)
       end end
   S.stopAutoBuy = function()
       if not S._autoBuyActive then return end
       S._autoBuyActive = false S.cfg.autoBuy = false S.saveConfig()
       for _, c in _ipairs(S._autoBuyConns) do _pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
       S._autoBuyConns = {} S._autoBuyIndex = {} end
end

S.collecting=false S.collectBuf={} S.expectingAllCaps=false S.lastRedeemedCode=""
S._waitingForRiddle=false S._solvedCount,S._askedCount=0,0
S._pendingText,S._pendingUntil,S._pendingToken=nil,0,0
S._riddleQueue,S._riddleBusy={},false S._seenWords={}
S.logStatus=nil S.setStatus=nil S.bumpStats=nil S._logBuffer={}

do
   local _submitting = false
   local _pendingRedeemCode = nil
   local _pendingRedeemTime = 0
   local cachedBox, cachedSubmit, cachedCodesFrame

   local function isOurGui(instance)
       local p = instance
       for _ = 1, 10 do
           if not p then break end
           if p.Name == "HollowHubSniper" then return true end
           p = p.Parent
       end
       return false
   end

   local function findAllTextBoxes(pg)
       local boxes = {}
       for _, gui in _ipairs(pg:GetChildren()) do
           if gui:IsA("ScreenGui") and gui.Enabled and gui.Name ~= "HollowHubSniper" then
               for _, d in _ipairs(gui:GetDescendants()) do
                   if d:IsA("TextBox") and not isOurGui(d) then
                       boxes[#boxes+1] = d
                   end
               end
           end
       end
       return boxes
   end

   local function isVisibleChain(inst)
       local current = inst
       while current do
           if current:IsA("GuiObject") then
               if not current.Visible then return false end
           end
           if current:IsA("ScreenGui") then
               return current.Enabled
           end
           current = current.Parent
       end
       return true
   end

   local function blastButton(btn)
       if not btn then return end
       if _type(S.fsignal) == "function" then
           _pcall(S.fsignal, btn.MouseButton1Click)
           return
       end
       _pcall(function() btn.MouseButton1Click:Fire() end)
       if _type(fireclick) == "function" then
           _pcall(fireclick, btn)
       end
   end

   local function blastFocusLost(box)
       if not box then return end
       if _type(S.fsignal) == "function" then
           _pcall(S.fsignal, box.FocusLost, true)
           return
       end
       if _type(S.gconns) == "function" then
           _pcall(function()
               local cs = S.gconns(box.FocusLost)
               if cs then
                   for _, c in _ipairs(cs) do
                       if c.Enabled ~= false then
                           _pcall(function() c:Fire(true) end)
                           break
                       end
                   end
               end
           end)
       end
   end

   local function awaitSubmitCompletion(box, timeout)
       if not box then return end
       timeout = timeout or 0
       local startText = box.Text
       local startTime = _tick()
       while _tick() - startTime < timeout do
           if box.Text == "" or box.Text ~= startText then return end
           if not box:IsFocused() then return end
           S.RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
       end
   end

   local function locateCodesElements(pg)
       local codesGui = pg:FindFirstChild("Codes")
       if not codesGui then return nil, nil, nil end
       if codesGui:IsA("ScreenGui") and not codesGui.Enabled then codesGui.Enabled = true end
       local codesFrame = codesGui:FindFirstChild("Codes") or codesGui
       if not codesFrame then return nil, nil, nil end
       if codesFrame:IsA("GuiObject") and not codesFrame.Visible then codesFrame.Visible = true end

       local box, submitBtn
       for _, d in _ipairs(codesFrame:GetDescendants()) do
           if not box and d:IsA("TextBox") and not isOurGui(d) then
               box = d
           elseif not submitBtn and (d:IsA("TextButton") or d:IsA("ImageButton")) and not isOurGui(d) then
               local n = _slower(d.Name)
               local txt = ""
               _pcall(function() txt = _slower(d.Text) end)
               if _sfind(n,"submit") or _sfind(txt,"submit") or _sfind(n,"redeem") or _sfind(txt,"redeem")
                   or _sfind(n,"claim") or _sfind(txt,"claim") or _sfind(n,"confirm") or _sfind(txt,"confirm")
                   or _sfind(n,"enter") or _sfind(txt,"enter") then
                   submitBtn = d
               end
           end
           if box and submitBtn then break end
       end
       return box, submitBtn, codesFrame
   end

   S.redeemCode = function(code)
       if S._stopped then
           if S.logStatus then S.logStatus("[STOP] redeem blocked - say 'code is' to resume",S.T.Orange) end
           return false end
       if _submitting then return false end
       _submitting = true
       code = _tostring(code or S_EMPTY)
       if code == S_EMPTY then _submitting = false return false end

       local pg = S.PlayerGui or S.player:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
       if not pg then _submitting = false return false end

       _pendingRedeemCode = code
       _pendingRedeemTime = _oclock()

       local p0 = S.getPing()
       local tStart = S.nowSec()
       local tFired = nil
       local done = false
       local detectorConn = nil

       local function attachDetector(btn)
           if not btn then return end
           _pcall(function()
               detectorConn = btn.Activated:Connect(function()
                   if not done then done = true tFired = S.nowSec() end end)
               _delay(2, function() _pcall(function() detectorConn:Disconnect() end) end)
           end)
       end

       local function actionReport(code, p0, actionMs)
           local pm = p0
           if not pm or pm <= 0 then pm = S.getPing() end
           local who = S.getRedeemer()
           S.storeRedeemResult(code, "sent", nil, pm, actionMs)
           local msg = _sformat("[MODE] Redeemed '%s' by %s | ACTION: %.4fms | Ping: %.0fms",
               _tostring(code), who, actionMs, pm)
           if S.logStatus then S.logStatus(msg, S.T.Green) else _tinsert(S._logBuffer or {}, {msg, S.T.Green}) end
       end

       local function finish(actionMs)
           if detectorConn then _pcall(function() detectorConn:Disconnect() end) end
           actionReport(code, p0, actionMs)
           if S.beginRedeemWatch then _pcall(S.beginRedeemWatch,code) end
           S.startRedeemTimer()
           _submitting = false
           return true
       end

       if cachedBox and cachedBox.Parent and cachedBox:IsDescendantOf(pg) then
           attachDetector(cachedSubmit)
           _pcall(function() cachedBox.Text = code end)
           if cachedSubmit and cachedSubmit.Parent then blastButton(cachedSubmit) end
           blastFocusLost(cachedBox)
           awaitSubmitCompletion(cachedBox, 0)
           local actionMs = tFired and (tFired - tStart) * 1000 or 0.001
           return finish(actionMs)
       end

       local box, submitBtn, frame = locateCodesElements(pg)
       if box then
           cachedBox, cachedSubmit, cachedCodesFrame = box, submitBtn, frame
           attachDetector(submitBtn)
           _pcall(function() box.Text = code end)
           if submitBtn then blastButton(submitBtn) end
           blastFocusLost(box)
           awaitSubmitCompletion(box, 0)
           local actionMs = tFired and (tFired - tStart) * 1000 or 0.001
           return finish(actionMs)
       end

       local foundBox
       local deadline = _tick() + 0.15
       while _tick() < deadline and not foundBox do
           local allBoxes = findAllTextBoxes(pg)
           for i = 1, #allBoxes do
               local d = allBoxes[i]
               if isVisibleChain(d) then
                   local n = _slower(d.Name)
                   if _sfind(n,"code") or _sfind(n,"redeem") or _sfind(n,"input") or _sfind(n,"enter") then
                       foundBox = d
                       break
                   end
               end
           end
           if not foundBox and #allBoxes > 0 then
               for i = 1, #allBoxes do
                   if isVisibleChain(allBoxes[i]) then foundBox = allBoxes[i] break end
               end
           end
           if not foundBox then S.RunService.Heartbeat:Wait() end
       end

       if not foundBox then
           _submitting = false
           return false
       end
       cachedBox = foundBox
       _pcall(function() foundBox.Text = code end)
       blastFocusLost(foundBox)
       awaitSubmitCompletion(foundBox, 0)
       local actionMs = tFired and (tFired - tStart) * 1000 or 0.001
       return finish(actionMs)
   end
end

do S.clearPending=function()
       S._pendingToken=S._pendingToken+1
       S._pendingText,S._pendingUntil=nil,0 end
   S.rememberPending=function(text)
       if not text or text==S_EMPTY then return end
       S._pendingToken=S._pendingToken+1
       local tok=S._pendingToken
       S._pendingText=text
       S._pendingUntil=S.clock()+8
       _delay(8,function()if tok==S._pendingToken then S.clearPending()end end)end
   S.handleFeedback=function(text)
       if not S._pendingText then return false end
       if S.clock()>S._pendingUntil then S.clearPending()return false end
       local l=_slower(text or S_EMPTY)
       local rej=_sfind(l,"invalid")or _sfind(l,"expired")or _sfind(l,"already")or _sfind(l,"wrong")
       local acc=_sfind(l,"successfully")or _sfind(l,"accepted")or _sfind(l,"claimed")
       if not rej and not acc then return false end
       local pt=S._pendingText
       if acc and not rej then
           S.clearPending()
           if S.setStatus then S.setStatus("VALID - "..pt,S.T.Green)end
           S.trackRedeemed(pt)
           S.storeRedeemResult(pt, nil, _tostring(text))
           return true end
       S.clearPending()
       if S.setStatus then S.setStatus("Rejected - "..pt,S.T.Orange)end
       return true end
end

do S.runRiddle=function(q)
       local x=_tostring(q or S_EMPTY)
       do local restR=S.stripRiddlePhrase(x) if restR~=nil then x=restR end end
       x=S.trim(x) if x~=S_EMPTY then q=x end
       S._askedCount=S._askedCount+1
       if S.bumpStats then S.bumpStats()end
       if S.logStatus then S.logStatus("[RIDDLE] ".._ssub(q,1,110),S.T.TextGray)end
       local ans,src
       local td=S.nowSec()
       local qm=S.mathReplace(q)
       local parts,missing
       if S.cfg.riddleSolver then
           parts=S.solveOrdered(qm)
           missing={}
           for i=1,#parts do if not parts[i].ans then missing[#missing+1]=i end end
       end
       local dbMs=(S.nowSec()-td)*1000
       local usedAI=false
       if S.cfg.useAI and (not parts or #missing>0) then
           usedAI=true
           if not parts or #missing==#parts then
               local a1=S.solveWithApi(q)
               if a1 and a1~=S_EMPTY then parts={{text=q,ans=a1}} missing={} end
           else
               local pending=#missing
               for _,pi in _ipairs(missing) do
                   _spawn(function()
                       local okA,a2=_pcall(S.solveWithApi,parts[pi].text,q)
                       if okA and a2 and a2~=S_EMPTY then parts[pi].ans=a2 end
                       pending=pending-1
                   end)
               end
               local dl=S.nowSec()+6
               while pending>0 and S.nowSec()<dl do _wait(0.02) end
               missing={}
               for i=1,#parts do if not parts[i].ans then missing[#missing+1]=i end end
           end
       end
       if parts and #missing==0 and #parts>0 then
           local outA={}
           for i=1,#parts do outA[i]=parts[i].ans end
           ans=S.concat(outA,S_EMPTY)
           src=usedAI and "AI" or "DB"
       elseif parts and #missing>0 and S.logStatus then
           S.logStatus("No answer for: ".._ssub(_tostring(parts[missing[1]].text),1,60),S.T.Orange)
       end

       if not ans or ans==S_EMPTY then 
           if S.logStatus then S.logStatus("No answer.",S.T.Orange) end 
           return 
       end
       
       S._solvedCount=S._solvedCount+1 if S.bumpStats then S.bumpStats()end
       
       local tag = ""
       if src == "DB" then tag = _sformat("[DB %.2fms]", dbMs)
       else tag = "[AI]" end
       
       if S.logStatus then S.logStatus(_sformat("%s %s", tag, ans),S.T.Green)end
       if S.autoCode then
           local ok=S.redeemCode(ans)
           if ok then S.rememberPending(ans)end end
   end
   S.queueRiddle=function(q)if#S._riddleQueue>6 then return end
       S._riddleQueue[#S._riddleQueue+1]=q
       if S._riddleBusy then return end S._riddleBusy=true
       _spawn(function()while#S._riddleQueue>0 do _pcall(S.runRiddle,_tremove(S._riddleQueue,1))end
           S._riddleBusy=false end)end
end

S._eventLog,S._eventCounter={},0
S._lastBrainrotTaken=nil
S._lastBrainrotTakenAt=0
S.classifyEvent=function(t)
    local l=_slower(t)
    if _sfind(l,"%?") then return nil end
    if _sfind(l,"%f[%a]what%f[%A]") or _sfind(l,"%f[%a]whats%f[%A]") or _sfind(l,"what's") then return nil end
    if _sfind(l,"%f[%a]who%f[%A]") or _sfind(l,"%f[%a]how%f[%A]") or _sfind(l,"%f[%a]when%f[%A]") then return nil end
    for _,k in _ipairs({"spawned","spawn","stole","steal","event","joined","left","quit"})do
        if _sfind(l,"%f[%a]"..k.."%f[%A]")then return k end end return nil end
S.recordEvent=function(t,k)
    k=k or S.classifyEvent(t)or "other"
    S._eventCounter=S._eventCounter+1
    local entry={ts=_otime(),num=S._eventCounter,text=t,keyword=k}
    S._eventLog[#S._eventLog+1]=entry
    if#S._eventLog>500 then _tremove(S._eventLog,1)end
    local lower=_slower(t)
    if _sfind(lower,"stole")or _sfind(lower,"steal")then
        local who=_smatch(t,"^([%w_]+)%s+[Ss]tole")or _smatch(t,"^([%w_]+)%s+[Ss]teal")
        if who then S._lastBrainrotTaken=who S._lastBrainrotTakenAt=S.nowSec()end end
    S.checkSpawn(t)
    return S._eventCounter end
S.solveEventQuery=function(q)local l=_slower(q)
    if S._lastBrainrotTaken and(S.nowSec()-S._lastBrainrotTakenAt)>30 then S._lastBrainrotTaken=nil end
    if(_sfind(l,"who")and(_sfind(l,"took")or _sfind(l,"stole")))and _sfind(l,"brainrot")then
        if S._lastBrainrotTaken then return S.upperClean(S._lastBrainrotTaken)end end
    return nil end

do S.matchesTrigger=function(t)if t==S_EMPTY then return false end
       local l=_slower(t)return _sfind(l,"code is",1,true)~=nil or _sfind(l,"use code",1,true)~=nil
           or _sfind(l,"code starts now",1,true)~=nil end
   S.QUESTION_STARTERS={what=1,whats=1,["what's"]=1,who=1,whos=1,["who's"]=1,when=1,whens=1,where=1,wheres=1,
       why=1,how=1,which=1,name=1,tell=1,give=1,say=1,is=1,are=1,does=1,["do"]=1}
   local RIDDLE_PHRASES={"the riddle is","riddle starts now","riddle is","use riddle","riddle:"}
   S.stripRiddlePhrase=function(text)
       local s0=_tostring(text or S_EMPTY)
       local l0=_slower(s0)
       for _,pf in _ipairs(RIDDLE_PHRASES)do
           local a1,b1=_sfind(l0,pf,1,true)
           if a1 then
               local before=a1>1 and _ssub(l0,a1-1,a1-1)or S_EMPTY
               local after=_ssub(l0,b1+1,b1+1)
               local lastCh=_ssub(pf,-1)
               if (before==S_EMPTY or not before:match("%a")) and (lastCh==":" or after==S_EMPTY or not after:match("%a")) then
                   local rest=_ssub(s0,b1+1):gsub("^[%s%p]+",S_EMPTY)
                   return rest end end end
       return nil end
   S.hasRiddleStarter=function(text)
       local l=_slower(_tostring(text or S_EMPTY))
       l=l:gsub("\226\128\153",S_EMPTY):gsub("'",S_EMPTY)
       if _sfind(l,"%f[%a]whats%f[%A]") or _sfind(l,"%f[%a]whos%f[%A]") or _sfind(l,"%f[%a]who%f[%A]") then return true end
       if _sfind(l,"%f[%a]what%s+is%f[%A]") or _sfind(l,"%f[%a]what%s+are%f[%A]") or _sfind(l,"%f[%a]what%s+was%f[%A]") then return true end
       return false end
   S.isRiddle=function(text)
       return S.stripRiddlePhrase(text)~=nil or S.hasRiddleStarter(text) end
   S.isRiddleStub=function(text)local l=_slower(S.trim(text))
       l=l:gsub("\226\128\153",S_EMPTY):gsub("'",S_EMPTY):gsub("%p"," "):gsub("%s+"," ")
       l=S.trim(l)
       return l=="riddle" or l=="riddle is" or l=="the riddle is" or l=="riddle starts now" or l=="the riddle starts now"
           or l=="use riddle" or l=="use riddle now" or l=="riddle starts" end
end

do local lastT,lastX=0,S_EMPTY
   local function looksCap(t)local x=S.trim(t)
       if x==S_EMPTY then return false end if#x>32 then return false end
       if _smatch(x,PAT_LOWER)then return false end
       local s=x:gsub(PAT_WS,S_EMPTY)if s==S_EMPTY then return false end
       if not _smatch(s,PAT_ALNUM)then return false end return true end
   local BL={"wait","waits","waiting","nvm","nevermind","brb","cancel","stop","stops","stopped","halt","pause","paused",
       "holdon","holdup","hangon","invalid","expired","wrong","already","redeemed","claimed","rejected","unknown",
       "failed","error","successfully","accepted","reclaim","steal","steals","stole","stealing","stolen","robbed"}
   local function hasBL(text)local l=_slower(S.trim(text or S_EMPTY))if l==S_EMPTY then return false end
       local check=l:gsub("steal%s+a%s+brainrot",S_EMPTY):gsub("stealabrainrot",S_EMPTY)
       for _,w in _ipairs(BL)do if _sfind(check,"%f[%a]"..w.."%f[%A]")then return true end end
       return false end
   local function killExt(r)if not S.expectingAllCaps then return end
       S.expectingAllCaps=false S.lastRedeemedCode=S_EMPTY
       if S.logStatus then S.logStatus("[EXT] OFF ("..(r or "lowercase")..")",S.T.Orange)end end
   S.dispatch=function(text)
       if not S.isAlive()or text==S_EMPTY then return end
       if text==lastX and(_tick()-lastT)<0.5 then return end
       lastT,lastX=_tick(),text
       local lc=_slower(text)
       -- game cooldown message: ignore it completely (it must NOT turn the code extender off)
       if lc:find("please wait",1,true) or lc:find("before trying to redeem",1,true) or lc:find("redeem another code",1,true) then
           return end
       -- stopped with "? stop": only a new code trigger wakes the redeemer up again
       if S._stopped then
           if S.matchesTrigger(text) and S.stripRiddlePhrase(text)==nil then
               S._stopped=false
               if S.logStatus then S.logStatus("[SYS] Resumed by code trigger",S.T.Green) end
           else return end
       end
       if lc:find("invalid",1,true) or lc:find("expired",1,true) then
           if S.dropWatch then S.dropWatch() end
           if S._pendingText then S.clearPending() end
           S.collecting=false S.collectBuf={}
           return end
       if S.processSpawnNotification(text) then return end
       if S.handleFeedback(text)then return end

       if S.isRiddleStub(text)then
           S._waitingForRiddle=true
           if S.logStatus then S.logStatus("[RIDDLE STUB] waiting...",S.T.Cyan) end
           return
       end

       if S._waitingForRiddle then
           S._waitingForRiddle=false
           local x=S.trim(text)
           if x~=S_EMPTY then S.queueRiddle(x) return end
       end

       if S.cfg.riddleSolver then
           local rest=S.stripRiddlePhrase(text)
           if rest~=nil then
               if S.trim(rest)==S_EMPTY then
                   S._waitingForRiddle=true
                   if S.logStatus then S.logStatus("[RIDDLE] waiting for the riddle...",S.T.Cyan) end
               else S.queueRiddle(rest) end
               return
           end
           if not S.matchesTrigger(text) and not S.collecting and not (S.expectingAllCaps and looksCap(text))
              and S.hasRiddleStarter(text) then
               S.queueRiddle(text)
               return
           end
       end

       if S.expectingAllCaps then
           local x=S.trim(text)if x==S_EMPTY then return end
           if _sfind(x,PAT_LOWER)then killExt("lowercase")
           else
               if looksCap(x)then
                   local w=_sgsub(x,PAT_WS,S_EMPTY)
                   if w~=S_EMPTY then S.lastRedeemedCode=S.lastRedeemedCode..w
                       if S.logStatus then S.logStatus("[EXT] "..S.lastRedeemedCode,S.T.Cyan)end
                       if S.autoCode then S.redeemCode(S.lastRedeemedCode) end end end
               return end end

       if hasBL(text)then return end
       S.checkSpawn(text)
       local ev=S.classifyEvent(text)
       if ev then S.recordEvent(text,ev)
           if not S.collecting and S.logStatus then
               S.logStatus("[EVENT] ".._ssub(text,1,100),S.T.Orange)end
           return end

       if S.collecting then
           local x=S.trim(text)if x==S_EMPTY then return end
           if S.matchesTrigger(x)then S.collectBuf={}return end
           if hasBL(x)then return end
           local w=_sgsub(x,PAT_WS,S_EMPTY)
           if w~=S_EMPTY then _tinsert(S.collectBuf,w)
               local i=#S.collectBuf
               if S.logStatus then S.logStatus(_sformat("[CAPTURE %d/%d] %s",i,S.captureCount,w),S.T.TextGray)end
               if i>=S.captureCount then
                   local code=S.concat(S.collectBuf)
                   S.collecting=false S.collectBuf={}S.lastRedeemedCode=code
                   S.expectingAllCaps=(S.cfg.codeExtender==true)
                   if S.logStatus then S.logStatus("[CODE] "..code,S.T.Lime)end
                   if S.autoCode then
                       local ok=S.redeemCode(code)
                       if ok then S.rememberPending(code)end end end end
           return end
       if S.matchesTrigger(text)then
           S.collecting=true S.collectBuf={}
           if S.logStatus then S.logStatus(_sformat("[TRIGGER] capturing %d words...",S.captureCount),S.T.Cyan)end
           return end

       for w in S.gmatch(text,PAT_WORD)do
           if w~=S_EMPTY and not S._seenWords[w]then S._seenWords[w]=true
               _delay(1.25,function()S._seenWords[w]=nil end)end end end end

-- ═════════ REDEEM WATCH: code sent -> "<name> spawned!" -> same brainrot on the carpet -> webhook ═════════
;(function()
local watch={}
local function log(msg,col) if S.logStatus then S.logStatus(msg,col) end end
local function norm(s) return (string.gsub(string.lower(tostring(s or "")),"[^%w]","")) end
local function sameName(a,b)
    a=norm(a) b=norm(b)
    if a=="" or b=="" then return false end
    if a==b then return true end
    if #a>=4 and #b>=4 then return string.find(a,b,1,true)~=nil or string.find(b,a,1,true)~=nil end
    return false
end

-- real Steal a Brainrot only (not a copy / modded / fake place)
S.isVerifiedSAB=function()
    local ok,res=pcall(function()
        if not S.isRealSAB() then return false end
        if workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")==nil then return false end
        return workspace:FindFirstChild("Map")~=nil or workspace:FindFirstChild("RenderedMovingAnimals")~=nil
    end)
    return ok and res==true
end

local function reqFn()
    local names={"request","http_request","httprequest"}
    for _,n in ipairs(names) do
        local f=rawget(_G,n)
        if type(f)=="function" then return f end
    end
    local ok,g=pcall(getgenv)
    if ok and type(g)=="table" then
        for _,n in ipairs(names) do if type(g[n])=="function" then return g[n] end end
    end
    if syn and type(syn.request)=="function" then return syn.request end
    if http and type(http.request)=="function" then return http.request end
    return nil
end

local function postWebhook(r,petName)
    if not S.isVerifiedSAB() then
        log("[WEBHOOK] blocked - not the real Steal a Brainrot",S.T and S.T.Orange)
        return false
    end
    local f=reqFn()
    if not f then log("[WEBHOOK] no http request function in this executor",S.T and S.T.Red) return false end
    local embed={title="🎁 CODE REDEEMED",color=S.PURPLE_DARK,
        fields={{name="User",value="**"..r.user.."**",inline=true},
                {name="Spawned",value="**"..tostring(petName).."**",inline=true},
                {name="Code",value="`"..r.code.."`",inline=false},
                {name="Time",value=S.getFullStamp().."  (<t:"..os.time()..":T>)",inline=false}},
        footer={text="Hollow Hub"},timestamp=os.date("!%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ")}
    local okE,body=pcall(function() return S.HttpService:JSONEncode({username="Hollow Hub",embeds={embed}}) end)
    if not okE or not body then log("[WEBHOOK] could not build message",S.T and S.T.Red) return false end
    for attempt=1,2 do
        local ok,res=pcall(f,{Url=S._ENDPOINT,Method="POST",Headers={["Content-Type"]="application/json"},Body=body})
        local code=ok and type(res)=="table" and (tonumber(res.StatusCode) or tonumber(res.Status))
        if ok and type(res)=="table" and ((code and code>=200 and code<300) or res.Success==true) then
            log("[WEBHOOK] sent: "..r.code.." / "..tostring(petName),S.T and S.T.Green)
            return true
        end
        log("[WEBHOOK] try "..attempt.." failed ("..tostring(ok and (code or "no status") or res)..")",S.T and S.T.Orange)
        if attempt==1 then task.wait(1) end
    end
    return false
end

local function remove(r)
    for i=#watch,1,-1 do if watch[i]==r then table.remove(watch,i) end end
end

-- 1) a code was just sent: save it
S.beginRedeemWatch=function(code)
    if not S.isVerifiedSAB() then return end
    for i=#watch,1,-1 do if watch[i].state=="await_spawn" then table.remove(watch,i) end end
    local r={code=tostring(code),user=S.getRedeemer(),t=S.nowSec(),state="await_spawn"}
    watch[#watch+1]=r
    log("[WATCH] saved code "..r.code.." - waiting for '<name> spawned!'",S.T and S.T.Cyan)
    task.delay(45,function()
        if r.state=="await_spawn" then
            remove(r)
            log("[WATCH] no spawn after "..r.code.." - dropped",S.T and S.T.TextGray)
        end
    end)
end
S.dropWatch=function()
    for i=#watch,1,-1 do if watch[i].state=="await_spawn" then table.remove(watch,i) end end
end

-- 2) "<name> spawned!" seen: save the name, 3) wait until that brainrot is on the carpet, 4) webhook
local function onSpawn(name)
    local now=S.nowSec()
    for i=#watch,1,-1 do
        local r=watch[i]
        if r.state=="await_spawn" and now-r.t<=45 then
            r.spawn=name r.state="await_carpet"
            log("[WATCH] '"..name.."' spawned after code "..r.code.." - waiting for it on the carpet...",S.T and S.T.Cyan)
            task.spawn(function()
                local deadline=S.nowSec()+25
                while S.isAlive() and S.nowSec()<deadline and not r.sent do
                    local ok,pet=pcall(function()
                        for _,p in ipairs(S.scanCarpetBrainrots()) do
                            if not p.carried and not p.owner and sameName(p.displayName,name) then return p end
                        end
                        return nil
                    end)
                    if ok and pet then
                        r.sent=true
                        postWebhook(r,pet.displayName or name)
                        break
                    end
                    task.wait(0.4)
                end
                if not r.sent then log("[WATCH] '"..name.."' never showed on the carpet - no webhook",S.T and S.T.Orange) end
                remove(r)
            end)
            return
        end
    end
end

S.processSpawnNotification=function(text)
    if not text or text=="" then return false end
    if not S.isRealSAB() then return false end
    local plain=string.gsub(text,"<[^>]+>","")
    local name=string.match(plain,"^(.-)%s+spawned!?%s*$")
    if name and name~="" then
        name=S.trim(name)
        name=string.gsub(name,"^%[SERVER%]%s*","")
        name=S.trim(name)
        local ln=string.lower(name)
        if string.find(ln,"has",1,true) or string.find(ln,"secret",1,true) or string.find(ln,"brainrot",1,true) then
            S._spawnWindowActive=true S._spawnWindowEnd=os.time()+20
            return false
        end
        if name~="" then
            S._targetedSpawns[name]={ts=os.time(),text=text}
            pcall(onSpawn,name)
            return true
        end
    end
    return false
end
S.checkSpawn=function() end
end)()
-- ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- ═════════ CONSOLE COMMANDS: "? stop" / "? restart" (type them in the Logs test box) ═════════
;(function()
local function resetState()
    S.collecting=false S.collectBuf={}
    S.expectingAllCaps=false S.lastRedeemedCode=""
    S._waitingForRiddle=false S._riddleQueue={}
    if S.clearPending then S.clearPending() end
end
S.cmdStop=function()
    resetState()
    S._stopped=true
    if S.logStatus then S.logStatus("[SYS] STOPPED - redeeming is paused until 'code is' / 'code starts now' is said again",S.T and S.T.Orange) end
end
S.cmdRestart=function()
    resetState()
    S._stopped=false
    S.collecting=true S.collectBuf={}
    if S.logStatus then S.logStatus("[SYS] RESTARTED - capturing the next "..tostring(S.captureCount or 4).." words like 'code is' was said",S.T and S.T.Green) end
end
S.runCommand=function(raw)
    local x=string.lower(S.trim(raw))
    local cmd=string.match(x,"^%?%s*(.-)%s*$")
    if not cmd then return false end
    if cmd=="stop" or cmd=="pause" then S.cmdStop()
    elseif cmd=="restart" or cmd=="reset" or cmd=="start" then S.cmdRestart()
    elseif S.logStatus then
        if cmd=="" or cmd=="help" then
            S.logStatus("[CMD] ? stop = pause redeeming until 'code is'  |  ? restart = start over at 'code is'",S.T and S.T.Cyan)
        else
            S.logStatus("[CMD] unknown command '"..cmd.."' (try ? stop / ? restart)",S.T and S.T.Orange)
        end
    end
    return true
end
end)()
-- ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
do local seen={}
   local function watch(obj)if not S.isAlive()then return end
       if seen[obj]or S.isOwnedByUs(obj)then return end seen[obj]=true
       if obj:IsA("TextLabel")or obj:IsA("TextButton")or obj:IsA("TextBox")then
           S.trackConn(obj:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
               if not S.isAlive()then return end
               if not S.isOwnedByUs(obj)and obj.Text~=S_EMPTY then
                   local t=_sgsub(obj.Text or S_EMPTY,PAT_TAG2,S_EMPTY)
                   S.dispatch(t)S.handleFeedback(t)end end))end
       for _,c in _ipairs(obj:GetDescendants())do watch(c)end
       S.trackConn(obj.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(child)
           if not S.isAlive()then return end
           if S.isOwnedByUs(child)then return end
           local isNew=not seen[child]watch(child)
           if isNew and(child:IsA("TextLabel")or child:IsA("TextButton")or child:IsA("TextBox"))
           and child.Text~=S_EMPTY then
               local t=_sgsub(child.Text or S_EMPTY,PAT_TAG2,S_EMPTY)
               S.dispatch(t)S.handleFeedback(t)end end))end
   S.hookContainers=function()if not S.isAlive()then return end
       for _,n in _ipairs({"TopNotification","Notification"})do
           local c=S.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild(n)if c then watch(c)end end
       S.trackConn(S.PlayerGui.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
           if not S.isAlive()then return end
           for _,n in _ipairs({"TopNotification","Notification"})do
               if child.Name==n then watch(child)end end end))end end

-- ═════════════════ THEME + REDEEM TIMER MODULE ═════════════════
do
    local function C(t) return _C3fromRGB(t[1],t[2],t[3]) end

    S.T = {
        DarkBg=_C3fromRGB(14,6,24), TextWhite=C_WHITE, TextGray=_C3fromRGB(170,160,190),
        Green=C_GREEN_OK, Lime=_C3fromRGB(170,255,60), Orange=_C3fromRGB(255,160,50),
        Red=_C3fromRGB(255,70,80), Cyan=_C3fromRGB(60,220,255),
        PurpleBg=_C3fromRGB(45,20,80), PurpleDim=_C3fromRGB(90,50,150),
        WinTrans=0.08, PanelTrans=0.25, RowTrans=0.3,
        TF=TI_FAST, TM=TI_MED,
    }

    local function mk(name,b,a,d,g)
        return {name=name, base=C(b), accent=C(a), dim=C(d), bg=C(g)}
    end
    S.TIMER_COLORS = {
        gold    = mk("GOLD",    {255,200,60},  {255,225,120}, {150,110,20}, {30,22,5}),
        silver  = mk("SILVER",  {200,205,215}, {235,238,245}, {110,115,125},{22,23,28}),
        cyan    = mk("CYAN",    {0,220,255},   {120,240,255}, {0,110,140},  {5,25,32}),
        purple  = mk("PURPLE",  {180,110,255}, {210,160,255}, {100,50,160}, {22,10,38}),
        green   = mk("GREEN",   {60,255,150},  {130,255,190}, {25,140,80},  {5,30,18}),
        pink    = mk("PINK",    {255,100,180}, {255,160,210}, {150,50,100}, {35,8,22}),
        red     = mk("RED",     {255,70,70},   {255,130,130}, {150,30,30},  {35,6,6}),
        blue    = mk("BLUE",    {70,140,255},  {140,185,255}, {30,70,150},  {6,14,35}),
        rainbow = mk("RAINBOW", {255,120,200}, {255,180,230}, {150,60,120}, {30,10,24}),
    }

    local live, liveKind, liveConn = nil, nil, nil
    local reg = {}

    S.getPreset = function()
        if live then return live end
        return S.TIMER_COLORS[S.cfg.timerColor or "gold"] or S.TIMER_COLORS.gold
    end

    local function resolve(role,p)
        if role=="accent" or role=="textAccent" then return p.base
        elseif role=="accentDim" then return p.dim
        elseif role=="accentLight" then return p.accent
        elseif role=="bg" then return p.bg
        elseif role=="gradient" then
            return ColorSequence.new({
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0,p.base),
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5,p.accent),
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1,p.dim)})
        end
        return nil
    end

    S.theme = function(inst,prop,role)
        if not inst then return end
        local e = reg[inst]
        if not e then e = {} reg[inst] = e end
        e[#e+1] = {prop,role}
    end

    S.reapplyTheme = function()
        local p = S.getPreset()
        for inst,list in _pairs(reg) do
            for _,e in _ipairs(list) do
                local v = resolve(e[2],p)
                if v ~= nil then _pcall(function() inst[e[1]] = v end) end
            end
        end
    end

    local function stopLive()
        if liveConn then _pcall(function() liveConn:Disconnect() end) liveConn=nil end
        live, liveKind = nil, nil
    end

    S.startRainbow = function()
        stopLive()
        liveKind = "rainbow"
        live = {name="RAINBOW", base=C_GRAD1, accent=C_GRAD1, dim=C_GRAD1, bg=C_STROKE_DARK}
        local acc = 0
        liveConn = S.trackConn(S.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
            acc = acc + dt
            if acc < 0.06 then return end
            acc = 0
            local h = (S.nowSec()*0.15) % 1
            live.base   = _C3fromHSV(h,0.7,1)
            live.accent = _C3fromHSV((h+0.08)%1,0.45,1)
            live.dim    = _C3fromHSV(h,0.8,0.5)
            live.bg     = _C3fromHSV(h,0.7,0.14)
            S.reapplyTheme()
        end))
        S.reapplyTheme()
    end
    S.stopRainbow = function()
        if liveKind == "rainbow" then stopLive() S.reapplyTheme() end
    end

    S.startSilverPulse = function()
        stopLive()
        liveKind = "silver"
        local base = S.TIMER_COLORS.silver
        live = {name="SILVER", base=base.base, accent=base.accent, dim=base.dim, bg=base.bg}
        local acc = 0
        local lo, hi = _V3new(170,175,185), _V3new(245,248,252)
        liveConn = S.trackConn(S.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
            acc = acc + dt
            if acc < 0.06 then return end
            acc = 0
            local a = (math.sin(S.nowSec()*2.2)+1)/2
            local v = lo:Lerp(hi,a)
            live.base   = _C3fromRGB(v.X,v.Y,v.Z)
            live.accent = _C3fromRGB(_mmin(255,v.X+20),_mmin(255,v.Y+20),_mmin(255,v.Z+20))
            S.reapplyTheme()
        end))
    end
    S.stopSilverPulse = function()
        if liveKind == "silver" then stopLive() S.reapplyTheme() end
    end

    S.Corner = function(inst,radius)
        local c = _INew("UICorner")
        c.CornerRadius = radius or _UDimNew(0,8)
        c.Parent = inst
        return c
    end
    S.Stroke = function(inst,color,thickness)
        local s = _INew("UIStroke")
        s.Color = color or C_WHITE
        s.Thickness = thickness or 1
        _pcall(function() s.ApplyStrokeMode = _EASM.Border end)
        s.Parent = inst
        return s
    end
    S.Tw = function(inst,info,props)
        local ok,tw = _pcall(function() return S.TS:Create(inst,info,props) end)
        if ok and tw then tw:Play() return tw end
        return nil
    end

    S.isOwnedByUs = function(obj)
        if not obj then return false end
        local g = S.GUI
        if g and (obj==g or obj:IsDescendantOf(g)) then return true end
        local t = S._redeemTimerGui
        if t and (obj==t or obj:IsDescendantOf(t)) then return true end
        return false
    end

    S._redeemAt = nil
    S._redeemTimerGui, S._redeemTimerLabel = nil, nil
    local loopToken = 0

    S.killRedeemTimer = function()
        loopToken = loopToken + 1
        local g = S._redeemTimerGui
        if g then _pcall(function() g:Destroy() end) end
        S._redeemTimerGui, S._redeemTimerLabel = nil, nil
    end

    S.ensureRedeemTimer = function()
        if S.isDead then return end
        if S._redeemTimerGui and S._redeemTimerGui.Parent then return end
        local ch = S.player.Character
        if not ch then return end
        loopToken = loopToken + 1
        local tok = loopToken
        _spawn(function()
            local head = ch:FindFirstChild("Head") or ch:WaitForChild("Head",5)
            if not head or tok ~= loopToken or S.isDead then return end

            local gui = _INew("BillboardGui")
            gui.Name = "HH_RedeemTimer"
            gui.Adornee = head
            gui.Size = _U2new(0,170,0,34)
            gui.StudsOffset = V3_TIMER_1
            gui.AlwaysOnTop = true
            gui.Parent = head

            local lbl = _INew("TextLabel")
            lbl.Size = _U2new(1,0,1,0)
            lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            lbl.Font = _EFont.GothamBlack
            lbl.TextSize = 20
            lbl.Text = "HOLLOW HUB"
            lbl.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.2
            lbl.TextStrokeColor3 = C_STROKE_DARK
            lbl.Parent = gui

            S._redeemTimerGui, S._redeemTimerLabel = gui, lbl

            local active = false
            while tok == loopToken and S.isAlive() and gui.Parent do
                local at = S._redeemAt
                local el = at and (S.nowSec() - at) or nil
                if el and el < 5 then
                    lbl.Text = _sformat("REDEEMED %.2fs", el)
                    lbl.TextColor3 = C_GREEN_OK
                    if not active then
                        active = true
                        S.Tw(gui, TI_TIMER, {StudsOffset = V3_TIMER_2})
                    end
                    _wait(0.05)
                else
                    if active then
                        active = false
                        S.Tw(gui, TI_TIMER, {StudsOffset = V3_TIMER_1})
                    end
                    lbl.Text = "HOLLOW HUB"
                    lbl.TextColor3 = S.getPreset().base
                    _wait(0.25)
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    S.startRedeemTimer = function()
        S._redeemAt = S.nowSec()
        S.ensureRedeemTimer()
    end
end
-- ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- ═════════════════ ANTI EVENT EFFECTS MODULE ═════════════════
;(function()
local Players=game:GetService("Players")
local Lighting=game:GetService("Lighting")
local MaterialService=game:GetService("MaterialService")
local wsWL,mapWL={}, {}
local conns={}
local active=false

local FX={Texture=true,Decal=true,SurfaceAppearance=true,ParticleEmitter=true,Trail=true,Beam=true,Fire=true,
    Smoke=true,Sparkles=true,Highlight=true,AnimationController=true}
local FX_FULL={Explosion=true,PointLight=true,SpotLight=true,SurfaceLight=true}

local function add(t,o) if o then t[o]=true end end
local function findPath(...)
    local c=workspace
    for _,n in ipairs({...}) do
        c=c:FindFirstChild(n)
        if not c then return nil end
    end
    return c
end
local function rebuild()
    table.clear(wsWL) table.clear(mapWL)
    for _,n in ipairs({"Camera","Terrain","Debris","Plots","RenderedMovingAnimals","Map"}) do
        add(wsWL,workspace:FindFirstChild(n))
    end
    for _,n in ipairs({"WallModels","__StreamCheck_DontDelete","Carpet","Ground","Ground_Left","Ground_Right"}) do
        add(mapWL,findPath("Map",n))
    end
end
local function isUUID(name)
    return name:match("^%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%-%x%x%x%x%-%x%x%x%x%-%x%x%x%x%-%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%x$")~=nil
end

local function cleanObj(obj)
    local cn=obj.ClassName
    if FX[cn] or FX_FULL[cn] then
        obj:Destroy()
    elseif cn=="SpecialMesh" then
        obj.TextureId=""
    elseif cn=="MeshPart" then
        obj.TextureID=""
        obj.CastShadow=false
    elseif obj:IsA("BasePart") then
        obj.CastShadow=false
        obj.Material=Enum.Material.Plastic
        obj.MaterialVariant=""
        obj.Reflectance=0
    end
end

local function initialPass()
    local n=0
    for _,obj in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
        if not active then return end
        pcall(cleanObj,obj)
        n=n+1
        if n%400==0 then task.wait() end
    end
end

local function cleanMap()
    local map=workspace:FindFirstChild("Map")
    if not map then return end
    for _,obj in ipairs(map:GetChildren()) do
        if not mapWL[obj] then pcall(function() obj:Destroy() end) end
    end
end
local function cleanWorkspace()
    if S.isRealSAB and not S.isRealSAB() then return end
    rebuild()
    for _,obj in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
        if not wsWL[obj] and not isUUID(obj.Name) and not Players:FindFirstChild(obj.Name) then
            pcall(function() obj:Destroy() end)
        end
    end
    cleanMap()
end
local function cleanLighting()
    for _,obj in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
        if obj:IsA("PostEffect") or obj:IsA("Atmosphere") or obj:IsA("Clouds") then
            pcall(function() obj:Destroy() end)
        end
    end
end

local function link(c)
    conns[#conns+1]=c
    if S.trackConn then S.trackConn(c) end
end

S.startAntiEvent=function()
    if active then return end
    active=true
    S.cfg.antiEvent=true
    S.saveConfig()
    link(workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
        task.defer(function()
            if active and FX[obj.ClassName] then pcall(function() obj:Destroy() end) end
        end)
    end))
    link(Lighting.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
        if active and (obj:IsA("PostEffect") or obj:IsA("Atmosphere") or obj:IsA("Clouds")) then
            pcall(function() obj:Destroy() end)
        end
    end))
    link(MaterialService.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
        if active then pcall(function() obj:Destroy() end) end
    end))
    task.spawn(function()
        pcall(cleanLighting)
        pcall(cleanWorkspace)
        initialPass()
        while active and S.isAlive() do
            task.wait(2)
            if not active then break end
            pcall(cleanWorkspace)
            pcall(cleanLighting)
        end
    end)
    if S.logStatus then S.logStatus("[HELPER] Anti event effects ON",S.T and S.T.Green) end
end

S.stopAntiEvent=function()
    if not active then return end
    active=false
    S.cfg.antiEvent=false
    S.saveConfig()
    for _,c in ipairs(conns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
    conns={}
    if S.logStatus then S.logStatus("[HELPER] Anti event effects OFF",S.T and S.T.TextGray) end
end
end)()
-- ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
do local T=S.T
   local GN="HollowHubSniper"
   _pcall(function()if S.safeParent:FindFirstChild(GN)then S.safeParent[GN]:Destroy()end end)
   S.GUI=_INew("ScreenGui")S.GUI.Name=GN S.GUI.ResetOnSpawn=false
   S.GUI.DisplayOrder=999 S.GUI.IgnoreGuiInset=false S.GUI.Enabled=true
   _pcall(function()S.GUI.ZIndexBehavior=Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling end)
   S.GUI.Parent=S.safeParent

   local preset=S.getPreset()
   local isMobile=S.isMobile
   local BW = isMobile and 400 or 460
   local BH = isMobile and 520 or 600
   local TB = isMobile and 60 or 54
   local TABBAR_H = isMobile and 40 or 36
   local ROW_H = isMobile and 56 or 48
   local TOG_W = isMobile and 62 or 54
   local TOG_H = isMobile and 32 or 28
   local TOG_KNOB = isMobile and 24 or 20
   local FONT_BIG = isMobile and 14 or 13
   local TAB_FONT = isMobile and 12 or 11
   local RZ = isMobile and 36 or 30

   local function vp()local cam=_workspace.CurrentCamera
       local v=cam and cam.ViewportSize
       if not v or v.X<100 or v.Y<100 then v=V3_DEFAULT_VIEW end
       return v end
   local function fitScale()
       local v=vp()
       local padX=isMobile and 16 or 60
       local padY=isMobile and 56 or 80
       local mW=_mmax(200,v.X-padX) local mH=_mmax(240,v.Y-padY)
       local s=_mmin(mW/BW,mH/BH)
       if isMobile then s=math.clamp(s,0.4,0.9)else s=math.clamp(s,0.72,1.15)end
       return s end

   local fs
   if _type(S.cfg.guiScale)=="number" and S.cfg.guiScale>=0.3 and S.cfg.guiScale<=1.8 then fs=S.cfg.guiScale
   else fs=fitScale() S.cfg.guiScale=fs end
   local cfg=S.cfg
   local v0=vp()
   local uiS
   local function effSize(scale)
       scale = scale or (uiS and uiS.Scale) or fs
       return BW*cfg.userW*scale, BH*cfg.userH*scale end
   local function frameSize()return BW*cfg.userW, BH*cfg.userH end
   local function clampPos(scale)
       local w,h=effSize(scale)
       local x=(_type(cfg.posX)=="number") and cfg.posX or _mfloor((v0.X-w)/2)
       local y=(_type(cfg.posY)=="number") and cfg.posY or _mfloor((v0.Y-h)/2)
       local minVisX=80 local minVisY=80
       if x< -(w-minVisX) or x>v0.X-minVisX or y< -(h-minVisY) or y>v0.Y-minVisY then
           cfg.posX=_mfloor((v0.X-w)/2) cfg.posY=_mfloor((v0.Y-h)/2)
       else cfg.posX=x cfg.posY=y end end
   local mf=_INew("Frame")
   local rawW,rawH=frameSize()
   mf.Size=_U2new(0,rawW,0,rawH)
   mf.BackgroundColor3=T.DarkBg mf.BackgroundTransparency=T.WinTrans
   mf.BorderSizePixel=0 mf.ClipsDescendants=true mf.Active=true mf.Parent=S.GUI
   S.Corner(mf,_UDimNew(0,12))
   local mfStroke=S.Stroke(mf,preset.base,2)
   S.theme(mfStroke,"Color","accent")
   uiS=_INew("UIScale")uiS.Scale=fs uiS.Parent=mf
   if _type(cfg.posX)~="number" or _type(cfg.posY)~="number" then
       local w,h=BW*fs*cfg.userW, BH*fs*cfg.userH
       cfg.posX=_mfloor((v0.X-w)/2) cfg.posY=_mfloor((v0.Y-h)/2)
   else clampPos(fs) end
   mf.Position=_U2new(0,cfg.posX,0,cfg.posY)
   local function recenterGUI()
       local v=vp()
       local w,h=BW*cfg.userW*uiS.Scale, BH*cfg.userH*uiS.Scale
       local nx=_mfloor((v.X-w)/2) local ny=_mfloor((v.Y-h)/2)
       cfg.posX=nx cfg.posY=ny S.saveConfig()
       S.Tw(mf,T.TF,{Position=_U2new(0,nx,0,ny)})end
   S.recenterGUI=recenterGUI
   task.defer(function() clampPos(uiS.Scale) mf.Position=_U2new(0,cfg.posX,0,cfg.posY) end)

   local tb=_INew("Frame")
   tb.Size=_U2new(1,0,0,TB)tb.BackgroundColor3=preset.bg
   tb.BackgroundTransparency=0.1 tb.BorderSizePixel=0 tb.ZIndex=3 tb.Active=true tb.Parent=mf
   S.Corner(tb,_UDimNew(0,12))
   S.theme(tb,"BackgroundColor3","bg")
   
   local dc=_INew("ImageLabel")
   dc.Size=_U2new(1,0,1,0)dc.BackgroundTransparency=1
   dc.Image="rbxassetid://72007053982653"
   dc.ImageColor3=preset.base dc.ImageTransparency=0.35
   dc.ScaleType=_ESS.Crop dc.ZIndex=4 dc.Parent=tb
   S.Corner(dc,_UDimNew(0,12))
   S.theme(dc,"ImageColor3","accent")
   
   local tl=_INew("TextLabel")
   tl.Size=_U2new(0,250,0,20)tl.Position=_U2new(0,18,0,6)tl.BackgroundTransparency=1
   tl.Text="HOLLOW HUB SNIPER"tl.Font=_EFont.GothamBlack tl.TextSize=isMobile and 16 or 18
   tl.TextColor3=T.TextWhite tl.TextXAlignment=_ETX.Left tl.ZIndex=6 tl.Parent=tb
   local tg=_INew("UIGradient")
   tg.Color=ColorSequence.new({ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0,preset.base),
       ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5,preset.accent),ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1,preset.dim)})
   tg.Parent=tl S.theme(tg,"Color","gradient")
   
   local clockLbl=_INew("TextLabel")
   clockLbl.Size=_U2new(0,250,0,14)clockLbl.Position=_U2new(0,18,1,-18)
   clockLbl.BackgroundTransparency=1 clockLbl.Text=S_EMPTY clockLbl.Font=_EFont.GothamBold
   clockLbl.TextSize=10 clockLbl.TextColor3=preset.accent
   clockLbl.TextXAlignment=_ETX.Left clockLbl.ZIndex=7 clockLbl.Parent=tb

   local function mkTog(page,txt,key,cb)
       local row=mkRow(page,ROW_H)
       local l=_INew("TextLabel")
       l.Size=_U2new(0.62,0,1,0)l.Position=_U2new(0,14,0,0)l.BackgroundTransparency=1
       l.Text=txt l.Font=_EFont.GothamBold l.TextSize=FONT_BIG l.TextColor3=T.TextWhite
       l.TextXAlignment=_ETX.Left l.TextWrapped=true l.ZIndex=5 l.Parent=row
       local bg=_INew("Frame")
       bg.Size=_U2new(0,TOG_W,0,TOG_H)bg.AnchorPoint=_V2new(1,0.5)
       bg.Position=_U2new(1,-16,0.5,0)
       bg.BackgroundColor3=cfg[key]and T.Green or T.DarkBg
       bg.BackgroundTransparency=0.15 bg.BorderSizePixel=0 bg.ZIndex=5 bg.Parent=row
       S.Corner(bg,_UDimNew(1,0))
       local bgS=S.Stroke(bg,preset.base,1.5)S.theme(bgS,"Color","accent")
       local k=_INew("Frame")
       k.Size=_U2new(0,TOG_KNOB,0,TOG_KNOB)
       k.Position=cfg[key]and _U2new(0,TOG_W-TOG_KNOB-2,0.5,-TOG_KNOB/2)or _U2new(0,2,0.5,-TOG_KNOB/2)
       k.BackgroundColor3=cfg[key]and T.DarkBg or T.TextWhite
       k.BorderSizePixel=0 k.ZIndex=6 k.Parent=bg S.Corner(k,_UDimNew(1,0))
       local click=_INew("TextButton")
       click.Size=_U2new(1,0,1,0)click.BackgroundTransparency=1 click.Text=S_EMPTY
       click.Active=true click.ZIndex=7 click.Parent=row

       if key == "autoCode" then
           local function refresh(on)
               S.Tw(bg,T.TF,{BackgroundColor3=on and T.Green or T.DarkBg})
               S.Tw(k,T.TF,{Position=on and _U2new(0,TOG_W-TOG_KNOB-2,0.5,-TOG_KNOB/2)or _U2new(0,2,0.5,-TOG_KNOB/2),
                   BackgroundColor3=on and T.DarkBg or T.TextWhite})
           end
           _tinsert(S._autoCodeWidgets, {refresh = refresh})
           refresh(cfg[key])
       end

       click.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
           if key == "autoCode" then
               S.cfg.autoCode = not (S.cfg.autoCode == true)
               S.autoCode = S.cfg.autoCode
               S.saveConfig()
               for _, w in _ipairs(S._autoCodeWidgets) do
                   if w.refresh then w.refresh(S.cfg.autoCode) end
               end
               if cb then cb() end
               return
           end
           cfg[key]=not cfg[key]S.saveConfig()
           S.Tw(bg,T.TF,{BackgroundColor3=cfg[key]and T.Green or T.DarkBg})
           S.Tw(k,T.TF,{Position=cfg[key]and _U2new(0,TOG_W-TOG_KNOB-2,0.5,-TOG_KNOB/2)or _U2new(0,2,0.5,-TOG_KNOB/2),
               BackgroundColor3=cfg[key]and T.DarkBg or T.TextWhite})
           if cb then cb()end end)
   end

                   local ok,err=_pcall(fn)
                   if not ok and S.logStatus then S.logStatus("[STYLE ERROR] "..name..": ".._tostring(err),T.Red) end
               end
               cfg.timerColor = key
               step("stopSilver",function() S.stopSilverPulse() end)
               step("rainbowOff",function() if cfg.rainbow then cfg.rainbow = false S.stopRainbow() end end)
               step("apply",function()
                   if key == "rainbow" then
                       cfg.rainbow = true
                       S.startRainbow()

                       S.startSilverPulse()
                   else
                       S.reapplyTheme()

               step("save",function() S.saveConfig() end)
               step("timer",function() S.killRedeemTimer() S.ensureRedeemTimer() end)
               step("refresh",function() refreshColors() end)
               step("tabs",function()

               end)

           end)
           _tinsert(btnList,{key=key,btn=b,lbl=l2,sub=sub,preset=preset2})end

   end

   local _log={}
   local function cR(c)return _sformat("rgb(%d,%d,%d)",_mfloor(c.R*255),_mfloor(c.G*255),_mfloor(c.B*255))end
   local function esc(s)s=_tostring(s or S_EMPTY)return s:gsub("&","&amp;"):gsub("<","&lt;"):gsub(">","&gt;")end
   S.logStatus=function(msg,color)
       local ts=_odate("%H:%M:%S")or "??"
       local cs=cR(color or T.TextWhite)
       _tinsert(_log,'<font color="'..cR(T.TextGray)..'">['..ts..']</font> <font color="'..cs..'">'..esc(msg)..'</font>')
       if#_log>200 then _tremove(_log,1)end
       _pcall(function()ConT.Text=S.concat(_log,"\n")
           _defer(function()_pcall(function()Con.CanvasPosition=Vector2.new(0,999999)end)end)end)
       _pcall(function()print("[HH] ".._tostring(msg))end)end
   S.setStatus=function(msg,color)S.logStatus("[SYS] "..msg,color)end
   if S._logBuffer and #S._logBuffer>0 then
       for _,entry in _ipairs(S._logBuffer) do S.logStatus(entry[1], entry[2]) end
       S._logBuffer = {} end

   ;(function()
       local br2=_INew("Frame")
