       if S.runCommand and S.runCommand(t) then tb2.Text=S_EMPTY return end
       S.logStatus("[TEST IN] "..t,preset.base)_pcall(S.dispatch,t)tb2.Text=S_EMPTY end
   tb2.FocusLost:Connect(function(enter)if enter then fireT()end end)

   do local p=pages[6]
       local h=_INew("TextLabel")
       h.Size=_U2new(1,0,0,18)h.BackgroundTransparency=1 h.Text="AUTO-CHASE MIN VALUE"
       h.Font=_EFont.GothamBlack h.TextSize=11 h.TextColor3=preset.base
       h.TextXAlignment=_ETX.Left h.ZIndex=4 h.Parent=p
       S.theme(h,"TextColor3","textAccent")
       local valRow=mkRow(p,isMobile and 48 or 44)
       local vl=_INew("UIListLayout",valRow)
       vl.FillDirection=_EFD.Horizontal vl.Padding=_UDimNew(0,6)
       vl.HorizontalAlignment=_EHA.Center vl.VerticalAlignment=_EVA.Center
       local valBtns={}
       local vals={{25e6,"25M+"},{50e6,"50M+"},{100e6,"100M+"}}
       local function refreshVal()
           for _,vb in _ipairs(valBtns)do
               local on=(S.cfg.chaseMinValue or 25e6)==vb.val
               vb.btn.BackgroundColor3=on and preset.base or T.DarkBg
               vb.btn.BackgroundTransparency=on and 0.15 or 0.5
               vb.lbl.TextColor3=on and T.DarkBg or T.TextGray
               local s=vb.btn:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
               if s then s.Color=on and preset.accent or preset.dim s.Thickness=on and 2.5 or 1.5 end end end
       for _,r in _ipairs(vals)do
           local b=_INew("TextButton")
           b.Size=_U2new(0.33,-4,1,-8)b.BackgroundColor3=T.DarkBg
           b.BackgroundTransparency=0.5 b.BorderSizePixel=0 b.Text=S_EMPTY
           b.AutoButtonColor=false b.ZIndex=5 b.Parent=valRow
           S.Corner(b,_UDimNew(0,8))
           local bs=_INew("UIStroke",b)bs.Color=preset.dim bs.Thickness=1.5 bs.ApplyStrokeMode=_EASM.Border
           S.theme(bs,"Color","accentDim")
           local lbl=_INew("TextLabel")
           lbl.Size=_U2new(1,0,1,0)lbl.BackgroundTransparency=1 lbl.Text=r[2]
           lbl.Font=_EFont.GothamBlack lbl.TextSize=16 lbl.TextColor3=T.TextGray lbl.ZIndex=6 lbl.Parent=b
           b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() S.cfg.chaseMinValue=r[1] S.saveConfig() refreshVal() end)
           _tinsert(valBtns,{btn=b,lbl=lbl,val=r[1]})end
       refreshVal()
       local aRow=mkRow(p,ROW_H)
       local al=_INew("TextLabel")
       al.Size=_U2new(0.62,0,1,0)al.Position=_U2new(0,14,0,0)
       al.BackgroundTransparency=1 al.Text="AUTO CHASE ON SPAWN"
       al.Font=_EFont.GothamBold al.TextSize=FONT_BIG al.TextColor3=T.TextWhite
       al.TextXAlignment=_ETX.Left al.ZIndex=5 al.Parent=aRow
       local abg=_INew("Frame")
       abg.Size=_U2new(0,TOG_W,0,TOG_H)abg.AnchorPoint=_V2new(1,0.5)
       abg.Position=_U2new(1,-16,0.5,0)
       abg.BackgroundColor3=(S.cfg.autoChase)and T.Green or T.DarkBg
       abg.BackgroundTransparency=0.15 abg.BorderSizePixel=0 abg.ZIndex=5 abg.Parent=aRow
       S.Corner(abg,_UDimNew(1,0))
       S.Stroke(abg,preset.base,1.5)
       local ak=_INew("Frame")
       ak.Size=_U2new(0,TOG_KNOB,0,TOG_KNOB)
       ak.Position=(S.cfg.autoChase)and _U2new(0,TOG_W-TOG_KNOB-2,0.5,-TOG_KNOB/2)or _U2new(0,2,0.5,-TOG_KNOB/2)
       ak.BackgroundColor3=(S.cfg.autoChase)and T.DarkBg or T.TextWhite
       ak.BorderSizePixel=0 ak.ZIndex=6 ak.Parent=abg S.Corner(ak,_UDimNew(1,0))
       local aclick=_INew("TextButton")
       aclick.Size=_U2new(1,0,1,0)aclick.BackgroundTransparency=1 aclick.Text=S_EMPTY
       aclick.Active=true aclick.ZIndex=7 aclick.Parent=aRow
       aclick.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
           S.cfg.autoChase=not S.cfg.autoChase S.saveConfig()
           S.Tw(abg,T.TF,{BackgroundColor3=S.cfg.autoChase and T.Green or T.DarkBg})
           S.Tw(ak,T.TF,{Position=S.cfg.autoChase and _U2new(0,TOG_W-TOG_KNOB-2,0.5,-TOG_KNOB/2)or _U2new(0,2,0.5,-TOG_KNOB/2),
               BackgroundColor3=S.cfg.autoChase and T.DarkBg or T.TextWhite})end)

       local br=_INew("Frame")
       br.Size=_U2new(1,0,0,isMobile and 36 or 32)br.BackgroundTransparency=1 br.ZIndex=4 br.Parent=p
       local bl=_INew("UIListLayout",br)
       bl.FillDirection=_EFD.Horizontal bl.Padding=_UDimNew(0,6)bl.SortOrder=_ESO.LayoutOrder
       local rb=_INew("TextButton")
       rb.Size=_U2new(0.5,-3,1,0)rb.BackgroundColor3=T.PurpleBg
       rb.BackgroundTransparency=0.4 rb.BorderSizePixel=0 rb.Text="REFRESH"
       rb.Font=_EFont.GothamBlack rb.TextSize=12 rb.TextColor3=preset.base
       rb.AutoButtonColor=false rb.ZIndex=5 rb.Parent=br
       S.Corner(rb,_UDimNew(0,8))
       S.theme(rb,"BackgroundColor3","bg")S.theme(rb,"TextColor3","textAccent")
       local sb2=rb:Clone()sb2.Position=_U2new(0.5,3,0,0)
       sb2.Text="STOP CHASE"sb2.TextColor3=T.Red sb2.Parent=br
       local ss=sb2:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")if ss then ss.Color=T.Red end
       local list=_INew("Frame")
       list.Size=_U2new(1,0,0,0)list.AutomaticSize=_EAutoSz.Y
       list.BackgroundTransparency=1 list.ZIndex=4 list.Parent=p
       local ll=_INew("UIListLayout",list)ll.Padding=_UDimNew(0,6)ll.SortOrder=_ESO.LayoutOrder
       local st=_INew("TextLabel")
       st.Size=_U2new(1,0,0,16)st.BackgroundTransparency=1 st.Text="Idle."
       st.Font=_EFont.Gotham st.TextSize=11 st.TextColor3=T.TextGray
       st.TextXAlignment=_ETX.Left st.ZIndex=4 st.Parent=p
       local function rebuild()
           for _,c in _ipairs(list:GetChildren())do
               if c:IsA("TextButton")or c:IsA("Frame")then c:Destroy()end end
           if not S.isRealSAB() then st.Text="Not in Steal a Brainrot."st.TextColor3=T.Orange return end
           local found
           local ok,err=_pcall(function()found=S.scanCarpetBrainrots()end)
           if not ok then st.Text="Scan error: ".._tostring(err)st.TextColor3=T.Red return end
           found=found or {}
           if#found==0 then st.Text="No brainrots on carpet."st.TextColor3=T.TextGray return end
           st.Text=_sformat("%d found",#found)st.TextColor3=preset.base
           for i,pet in _ipairs(found)do
               if pet.part and pet.part.Parent then
                   local row=_INew("TextButton")
                   row.Size=_U2new(1,0,0,isMobile and 56 or 46)
                   row.BackgroundColor3=pet.carried and C_CARRIED_BG or T.PurpleBg
                   row.BackgroundTransparency=0.45 row.BorderSizePixel=0 row.Text=S_EMPTY
                   row.AutoButtonColor=false row.LayoutOrder=i row.ZIndex=5 row.Parent=list
                   S.Corner(row,_UDimNew(0,8))S.Stroke(row,T.PurpleDim,1.5)
                   local n=_INew("TextLabel")
                   n.Size=_U2new(1,-20,0,16)n.Position=_U2new(0,10,0,4)
                   n.BackgroundTransparency=1 n.Text=pet.displayName
                   n.Font=_EFont.GothamBold n.TextSize=13 n.TextColor3=T.TextWhite
                   n.TextXAlignment=_ETX.Left n.TextTruncate=_ETT.AtEnd n.ZIndex=6 n.Parent=row
                   local vs
                   if pet.value>=1e12 then vs=_sformat("$%.2fT/s",pet.value/1e12)
                   elseif pet.value>=1e9 then vs=_sformat("$%.2fB/s",pet.value/1e9)
                   elseif pet.value>=1e6 then vs=_sformat("$%.2fM/s",pet.value/1e6)
                   elseif pet.value>=1e3 then vs=_sformat("%.2fK/s",pet.value/1e3)
                   else vs=_sformat("$%.0f/s",pet.value)end
                   local inf=_INew("TextLabel")
                   inf.Size=_U2new(1,-20,0,14)inf.Position=_U2new(0,10,0,22)
                   inf.BackgroundTransparency=1
                   inf.Text=_sformat("%s · %d studs",vs,_mfloor(pet.distance))
                   inf.Font=_EFont.Gotham inf.TextSize=11 inf.TextColor3=T.TextGray
                   inf.TextXAlignment=_ETX.Left inf.ZIndex=6 inf.Parent=row
                   local target=pet.part
                   row.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                       if not target or not target.Parent then return end
                       S.chaseTo(target)end)
               end end end
       rb.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() _spawn(function()_wait(0.05)_pcall(rebuild)end)end)
       sb2.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()S.stopChase()end)
       _spawn(function()while S.isAlive()do _wait(1.2)
           if S.isAlive()and p.Visible then _pcall(rebuild)end end end)
   end

   local sl=_INew("TextLabel")
   sl.Size=_U2new(1,-16,0,20)sl.Position=_U2new(0,8,1,-26)
   sl.BackgroundTransparency=1 sl.Text="0 SOLVED // 0 ASKED"
   sl.Font=_EFont.GothamBlack sl.TextSize=11 sl.TextColor3=preset.base sl.ZIndex=4 sl.Parent=mf
   S.theme(sl,"TextColor3","textAccent")

   local rz=_INew("TextButton")
   rz.Size=_U2new(0,RZ,0,RZ)
   rz.Position=_U2new(1,-RZ,1,-RZ)
   rz.AnchorPoint=_V2new(0,0)
   rz.BackgroundColor3=preset.dim rz.BackgroundTransparency=0.2
   rz.BorderSizePixel=0 rz.Text="◢"
   rz.Font=_EFont.GothamBlack rz.TextSize=_mfloor(RZ*0.6)
   rz.TextColor3=preset.base rz.AutoButtonColor=false
   rz.ZIndex=25 rz.Parent=mf
   S.Corner(rz,_UDimNew(0,6))
   local rzs=S.Stroke(rz,preset.base,2)
   S.theme(rz,"TextColor3","textAccent")
   S.theme(rz,"BackgroundColor3","accentDim")
   S.theme(rzs,"Color","accent")
   local rzActive=false
   local rzStart=_V2new(0,0)local rzWStart=1 local rzHStart=1
   local rzInfo=_INew("TextLabel")
   rzInfo.Size=_U2new(0,150,0,22)rzInfo.Position=_U2new(0.5,-75,0,6)
   rzInfo.BackgroundColor3=T.DarkBg rzInfo.BorderSizePixel=0 rzInfo.Text=""
   rzInfo.Font=_EFont.GothamBold rzInfo.TextSize=12 rzInfo.TextColor3=preset.base
   rzInfo.Visible=false rzInfo.ZIndex=26 rzInfo.Parent=mf
   S.Corner(rzInfo,_UDimNew(0,6))
   rz.InputBegan:Connect(function(inp)
       if inp.UserInputType~=_EIT.MouseButton1 and inp.UserInputType~=_EIT.Touch then return end
       rzActive=true
       rzStart=_V2new(inp.Position.X,inp.Position.Y)
       rzWStart=cfg.userW rzHStart=cfg.userH
       rzInfo.Visible=true
       rzInfo.Text=_sformat("%d x %d",_mfloor(BW*cfg.userW),_mfloor(BH*cfg.userH))end)

   local dragActive=false
   local dragStart=_V2new(0,0)local dragOrigin=_V2new(0,0)
   local function beginDrag(inp)
       if dragActive or rzActive then return end
       if inp.UserInputType~=_EIT.MouseButton1 and inp.UserInputType~=_EIT.Touch then return end
       dragActive=true
       dragStart=_V2new(inp.Position.X,inp.Position.Y)
       dragOrigin=_V2new(mf.Position.X.Offset,mf.Position.Y.Offset)end
   tb.InputBegan:Connect(function(inp)
       if inp.UserInputType==_EIT.MouseButton1 or inp.UserInputType==_EIT.Touch then beginDrag(inp) end end)

   S.UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(inp)
       if rzActive then
           if inp.UserInputType~=_EIT.MouseMovement and inp.UserInputType~=_EIT.Touch then return end
           local cur=_V2new(inp.Position.X,inp.Position.Y)
           local dx=cur.X-rzStart.X local dy=cur.Y-rzStart.Y
           local sc=uiS.Scale
           local newW=rzWStart*BW + dx/sc local newH=rzHStart*BH + dy/sc
           newW=math.clamp(newW, 240, 1600) newH=math.clamp(newH, 200, 1600)
           cfg.userW=newW/BW cfg.userH=newH/BH
           mf.Size=_U2new(0,BW*cfg.userW,0,BH*cfg.userH)
           rzInfo.Text=_sformat("%d x %d",_mfloor(newW),_mfloor(newH))
           return end
       if dragActive then
           if inp.UserInputType~=_EIT.MouseMovement and inp.UserInputType~=_EIT.Touch then return end
           local cur=_V2new(inp.Position.X,inp.Position.Y)
           local d=cur-dragStart
           local nx=dragOrigin.X+d.X local ny=dragOrigin.Y+d.Y
           local v=vp() local w,h=effSize()
           local minX=-(w-80) local maxX=v.X-80
           local minY=0 local maxY=v.Y-60
           if nx<minX then nx=minX end if nx>maxX then nx=maxX end
           if ny<minY then ny=minY end if ny>maxY then ny=maxY end
           mf.Position=_U2new(0,nx,0,ny) end end)

   S.UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(inp)
       if inp.UserInputType==_EIT.MouseButton1 or inp.UserInputType==_EIT.Touch then
           if dragActive then dragActive=false cfg.posX=mf.Position.X.Offset cfg.posY=mf.Position.Y.Offset S.saveConfig() end
           if rzActive then rzActive=false rzInfo.Visible=false cfg.posX=mf.Position.X.Offset cfg.posY=mf.Position.Y.Offset S.saveConfig() end
       end end)

   local function SetTab(i)
       cfg.activeTab=i S.saveConfig()
       for j,pg in _ipairs(pages)do pg.Visible=(j==i)end
       local p=S.getPreset()
       for j,t in _ipairs(tabs)do
           t.btn.TextColor3=(j==i)and p.base or T.TextGray
           t.btn.BackgroundTransparency=(j==i)and 0.2 or 0.5
           t.stroke.Color=(j==i)and p.base or p.dim end end
   if _type(cfg.activeTab)~="number"or cfg.activeTab<1 or cfg.activeTab>6 then cfg.activeTab=1 end
   SetTab(cfg.activeTab)
   for i,t in _ipairs(tabs)do t.btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()SetTab(i)end)end

   local isMin=cfg.minimized
   local function applyMin(inst)
       local th=isMin and TB or BH
       if inst then mf.Size=_U2new(0,BW*cfg.userW,0,th)
       else S.Tw(mf,T.TM,{Size=_U2new(0,BW*cfg.userW,0,th)})end
       mn.Text=isMin and "+" or "-"
       tbar.Visible=not isMin ca.Visible=not isMin sl.Visible=not isMin
       rz.Visible=not isMin end
   applyMin(true)
   mn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
       isMin=not isMin cfg.minimized=isMin S.saveConfig()applyMin(false)end)

   S.UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(inp,proc)
       if proc then return end
       if inp.KeyCode==_EKC.RightControl then S.GUI.Enabled=not S.GUI.Enabled end end)
   _defer(function()
       S.logStatus("Code Sniper activated (v43.5)",preset.base)
       if S.cfg.rainbow then S.startRainbow()
       elseif S.cfg.timerColor == "silver" then S.startSilverPulse() end
   end)
end

do
    _pcall(function()
        local c=S.player.Character
        if c then for _,d in _ipairs(c:GetDescendants())do
            if d:IsA("BillboardGui")and d.Name=="HH_RedeemTimer"then
                _pcall(function()d:Destroy()end)end end end end)
    S.killRedeemTimer()
    _pcall(function()
        S.player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(ch)
            _wait(0.35)
            S._redeemTimerGui=nil S._redeemTimerLabel=nil
            S.ensureRedeemTimer()end)
    end)
    S.ensureRedeemTimer()
    S.hookContainers()
    if S.cfg.antiRagdoll then _spawn(function()_wait(1)_pcall(S.startAntiRagdoll)end)end
    if S.cfg.antiLag then _spawn(function()_wait(1.2)_pcall(S.startAntiLag)end)end
    if S.cfg.antiEvent then _spawn(function()_wait(1.4)_pcall(S.startAntiEvent)end)end
    S.updateAnchor()
    _spawn(function()_wait(2)_pcall(S.buildIndex,true)end)
    if S.cfg.autoBuy and S.startAutoBuy then _spawn(function()_wait(1.5)_pcall(S.startAutoBuy)end)end
end
